# 2.Go代码测试_test.go

## Go代码测试

\
#### USER

使用golang + wire + gin + gorm 构建一个http脚手架，并按照api  service repo 分层

#### ASSISTANT

我来帮你构建一个基于 Golang + Wire + Gin + GORM 的 HTTP 脚手架，按照 API、Service、Repository 三层架构设计。

**go.mod**

```go
module your-project

go 1.21

require (
    github.com/gin-gonic/gin v1.9.1
    github.com/google/wire v0.5.0
    gorm.io/gorm v1.25.5
```

**internal/model/user.go**

```go
package model

import (
    "time"
    "gorm.io/gorm"
)

type User struct {
    ID        uint           `json:"id" gorm:"primarykey"`
    Name      string         `json:"name" gorm:"not null"`
    Email     string         `json:"email" gorm:"uniqueIndex;not null"`
    CreatedAt time.Time      `json:"created_at"`
    UpdatedAt time.Time      `json:"updated_at"`
    DeletedAt gorm.DeletedAt `json:"-" gorm:"index"`
}

type CreateUserRequest struct {
    Name  string `json:"name" binding:"required"`
    Email string `json:"email" binding:"required,email"`
}

type UpdateUserRequest struct {
    Name  string `json:"name"`
    Email string `json:"email" binding:"omitempty,email"`
}
```

**internal/config/config.go**

```go
package config

import (
    "github.com/spf13/viper"
)

type Config struct {
    Server   ServerConfig   `mapstructure:"server"`
    Database DatabaseConfig `mapstructure:"database"`
}

type ServerConfig struct {
    Port string `mapstructure:"port"`
    Mode string `mapstructure:"mode"`
}

type DatabaseConfig struct {
    Host     string `mapstructure:"host"`
    Port     string `mapstructure:"port"`
    User     string `mapstructure:"user"`
    Password string `mapstructure:"password"`
    DBName   string `mapstructure:"dbname"`
}

func LoadConfig() (*Config, error) {
    viper.SetConfigName("config")
    viper.SetConfigType("yaml")
    viper.AddConfigPath(".")
    viper.AddConfigPath("./configs")
    
    // 设置默认值
    viper.SetDefault("server.port", "8080")
    viper.SetDefault("server.mode", "debug")
    viper.SetDefault("database.host", "localhost")
    viper.SetDefault("database.port", "3306")
    
    if err := viper.ReadInConfig(); err != nil {
        return nil, err
    }
    
    var config Config
    if err := viper.Unmarshal(&config); err != nil {
        return nil, err
    }
    
    return &config, nil
}
```

**pkg/database/database.go**

```go
package database

import (
    "fmt"
    "your-project/internal/config"
    "your-project/internal/model"
    
    "gorm.io/driver/mysql"
    "gorm.io/gorm"
    "gorm.io/gorm/logger"
)

func NewDatabase(cfg *config.Config) (*gorm.DB, error) {
    dsn := fmt.Sprintf("%s:%s@tcp(%s:%s)/%s?charset=utf8mb4&parseTime=True&loc=Local",
        cfg.Database.User,
        cfg.Database.Password,
        cfg.Database.Host,
        cfg.Database.Port,
        cfg.Database.DBName,
    )
    
    db, err := gorm.Open(mysql.Open(dsn), &gorm.Config{
        Logger: logger.Default.LogMode(logger.Info),
    })
    if err != nil {
        return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to connect database: %w", err)
    }
    
    // 自动迁移
    if err := db.AutoMigrate(&model.User{}); err != nil {
        return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to migrate database: %w", err)
    }
    
    return db, nil
}
```

**internal/repository/user_repository.go**

```go
package repository

import (
    "your-project/internal/model"
    "gorm.io/gorm"
)

type UserRepository interface {
    Create(user *model.User) error
    GetByID(id uint) (*model.User, error)
    GetByEmail(email string) (*model.User, error)
    Update(user *model.User) error
    Delete(id uint) error
    List(offset, limit int) ([]*model.User, error)
}

type userRepository struct {
    db *gorm.DB
}

func NewUserRepository(db *gorm.DB) UserRepository {
    return &userRepository{db: db}
}

func (r *userRepository) Create(user *model.User) error {
    return r.db.Create(user).Error
}

func (r *userRepository) GetByID(id uint) (*model.User, error) {
    var user model.User
    err := r.db.First(&user, id).Error
    if err != nil {
        return nil, err
    }
    return &user, nil
}

func (r *userRepository) GetByEmail(email string) (*model.User, error) {
    var user model.User
    err := r.db.Where("email = ?", email).First(&user).Error
    if err != nil {
        return nil, err
    }
    return &user, nil
}

func (r *userRepository) Update(user *model.User) error {
    return r.db.Save(user).Error
}

func (r *userRepository) Delete(id uint) error {
    return r.db.Delete(&model.User{}, id).Error
}

func (r *userRepository) List(offset, limit int) ([]*model.User, error) {
    var users []*model.User
    err := r.db.Offset(offset).Limit(limit).Find(&users).Error
    return users, err
}
```

**internal/service/user_service.go**

```go
package service

import (
    "errors"
    "your-project/internal/model"
    "your-project/internal/repository"
    "gorm.io/gorm"
)

type UserService interface {
    CreateUser(req *model.CreateUserRequest) (*model.User, error)
    GetUser(id uint) (*model.User, error)
    UpdateUser(id uint, req *model.UpdateUserRequest) (*model.User, error)
    DeleteUser(id uint) error
    ListUsers(page, pageSize int) ([]*model.User, error)
}

type userService struct {
    userRepo repository.UserRepository
}

func NewUserService(userRepo repository.UserRepository) UserService {
    return &userService{
        userRepo: userRepo,
    }
}

func (s *userService) CreateUser(req *model.CreateUserRequest) (*model.User, error) {
    // 检查邮箱是否已存在
    if _, err := s.userRepo.GetByEmail(req.Email); err == nil {
        return nil, errors.New("email already exists")
    }
    
    user := &model.User{
        Name:  req.Name,
        Email: req.Email,
    }
    
    if err := s.userRepo.Create(user); err != nil {
        return nil, err
    }
    
    return user, nil
}

func (s *userService) GetUser(id uint) (*model.User, error) {
    return s.userRepo.GetByID(id)
}

func (s *userService) UpdateUser(id uint, req *model.UpdateUserRequest) (*model.User, error) {
    user, err := s.userRepo.GetByID(id)
    if err != nil {
        return nil, err
    }
    
    if req.Name != "" {
        user.Name = req.Name
    }
    if req.Email != "" {
        // 检查新邮箱是否已被其他用户使用
        if existingUser, err := s.userRepo.GetByEmail(req.Email); err == nil && existingUser.ID != id {
            return nil, errors.New("email already exists")
        }
        user.Email = req.Email
    }
    
    if err := s.userRepo.Update(user); err != nil {
        return nil, err
    }
    
    return user, nil
}

func (s *userService) DeleteUser(id uint) error {
    if _, err := s.userRepo.GetByID(id); err != nil {
        if errors.Is(err, gorm.ErrRecordNotFound) {
            return errors.New("user not found")
        }
        return err
    }
    
    return s.userRepo.Delete(id)
}

func (s *userService) ListUsers(page, pageSize int) ([]*model.User, error) {
    offset := (page - 1) * pageSize
    return s.userRepo.List(offset, pageSize)
}
```

**internal/api/handler/user_handler.go**

```go
package handler

import (
    "net/http"
    "strconv"
    "your-project/internal/model"
    "your-project/internal/service"
    
    "github.com/gin-gonic/gin"
)

type UserHandler struct {
    userService service.UserService
}

func NewUserHandler(userService service.UserService) *UserHandler {
    return &UserHandler{
        userService: userService,
    }
}

func (h *UserHandler) CreateUser(c *gin.Context) {
    var req model.CreateUserRequest
    if err := c.ShouldBindJSON(&req); err != nil {
        c.JSON(http.StatusBadRequest, gin.H{"error": err.Error()})
        return
    }
    
    user, err := h.userService.CreateUser(&req)
    if err != nil {
        c.JSON(http.StatusBadRequest, gin.H{"error": err.Error()})
        return
    }
    
    c.JSON(http.StatusCreated, gin.H{"data": user})
}

func (h *UserHandler) GetUser(c *gin.Context) {
    idStr := c.Param("id")
    id, err := strconv.ParseUint(idStr, 10, 32)
    if err != nil {
        c.JSON(http.StatusBadRequest, gin.H{"error": "invalid user id"})
        return
    }
    
    user, err := h.userService.GetUser(uint(id))
    if err != nil {
        c.JSON(http.StatusNotFound, gin.H{"error": "user not found"})
        return
    }
    
    c.JSON(http.StatusOK, gin.H{"data": user})
}

func (h *UserHandler) UpdateUser(c *gin.Context) {
    idStr := c.Param("id")
    id, err := strconv.ParseUint(idStr, 10, 32)
    if err != nil {
        c.JSON(http.StatusBadRequest, gin.H{"error": "invalid user id"})
        return
    }
    
    var req model.UpdateUserRequest
    if err := c.ShouldBindJSON(&req); err != nil {
        c.JSON(http.StatusBadRequest, gin.H{"error": err.Error()})
        return
    }
    
    user, err := h.userService.UpdateUser(uint(id), &req)
    if err != nil {
        c.JSON(http.StatusBadRequest, gin.H{"error": err.Error()})
        return
    }
    
    c.JSON(http.StatusOK, gin.H{"data": user})
}

func (h *UserHandler) DeleteUser(c *gin.Context) {
    idStr := c.Param("id")
    id, err := strconv.ParseUint(idStr, 10, 32)
    if err != nil {
        c.JSON(http.StatusBadRequest, gin.H{"error": "invalid user id"})
        return
    }
    
    if err := h.userService.DeleteUser(uint(id)); err != nil {
        c.JSON(http.StatusBadRequest, gin.H{"error": err.Error()})
        return
    }
    
    c.JSON(http.StatusOK, gin.H{"message": "user deleted successfully"})
}

func (h *UserHandler) ListUsers(c *gin.Context) {
    pageStr := c.DefaultQuery("page", "1")
    pageSizeStr := c.DefaultQuery("page_size", "10")
    
    page, _ := strconv.Atoi(pageStr)
    pageSize, _ := strconv.Atoi(pageSizeStr)
    
    if page < 1 {
        page = 1
    }
    if pageSize < 1 || pageSize > 100 {
        pageSize = 10
    }
    
    users, err := h.userService.ListUsers(page, pageSize)
    if err != nil {
        c.JSON(http.StatusInternalServerError, gin.H{"error": err.Error()})
        return
    }
    
    c.JSON(http.StatusOK, gin.H{"data": users})
}
```

**internal/api/router/router.go**

```go
package router

import (
    "your-project/internal/api/handler"
    
    "github.com/gin-gonic/gin"
)

func SetupRouter(userHandler *handler.UserHandler) *gin.Engine {
    r := gin.Default()
    
    // 健康检查
    r.GET("/health", func(c *gin.Context) {
        c.JSON(200, gin.H{"status": "ok"})
    })
    
    // API 路由组
    api := r.Group("/api/v1")
    {
        users := api.Group("/users")
        {
            users.POST("", userHandler.CreateUser)
            users.GET("/:id", userHandler.GetUser)
            users.PUT("/:id", userHandler.UpdateUser)
            users.DELETE("/:id", userHandler.DeleteUser)
            users.GET("", userHandler.ListUsers)
        }
    }
    
    return r
}
```

**wire/wire.go**

```go
//go:build wireinject
// +build wireinject

package wire

import (
    "your-project/internal/api/handler"
    "your-project/internal/api/router"
    "your-project/internal/config"
    "your-project/internal/repository"
    "your-project/internal/service"
    "your-project/pkg/database"
    
    "github.com/gin-gonic/gin"
    "github.com/google/wire"
)

func InitializeApp() (*gin.Engine, error) {
    wire.Build(
        config.LoadConfig,
        database.NewDatabase,
        repository.NewUserRepository,
        service.NewUserService,
        handler.NewUserHandler,
        router.SetupRouter,
    )
    return &gin.Engine{}, nil
}
```

```go
    if err != nil {
```

### 使用说明

首先安装依赖并生成 Wire 代码：

```bash
go mod tidy
go install github.com/google/wire/cmd/wire@latest
cd wire && wire
```

创建配置文件 `config.yaml`：

```yaml
server:
  port: "8080"
  mode: "debug"

database:
  host: "localhost"
  port: "3306"
  user: "root"
  password: "password"
  dbname: "your_database"
```

运行服务：

```bash
go run cmd/server/main.go
```

这个脚手架实现了完整的三层架构，包含用户的 CRUD 操作，使用 Wire 进行依赖注入，具有良好的可扩展性和可维护性。你可以基于这个结构添加更多的业务模块和功能。

#### USER

2025/07/21 22:10:55 /Users/fatcat/workspace/go/src/github.com/featcat/wire-demo/pkg/database/database.go:30 Error 1170 (42000): BLOB/TEXT column 'email' used in key specification without a key length \[8.154ms\] \[rows:0\] CREATE TABLE `users` (`id` bigint unsigned AUTO_INCREMENT,`name` longtext NOT NULL,`email` longtext NOT NULL,`created_at` datetime(3) NULL,`updated_at` datetime(3) NULL,`deleted_at` datetime(3) NULL,PRIMARY KEY (`id`),UNIQUE INDEX `idx_users_email` (`email`),INDEX `idx_users_deleted_at` (`deleted_at`)) 2025/07/21 22:10:55 Failed to initialize app:failed to migrate database: Error 1170 (42000): BLOB/TEXT column 'email' used in key specification without a key length exit status 1

#### ASSISTANT

这个错误是因为 MySQL 在为 `longtext` 类型的字段创建索引时需要指定键长度。问题出现在 User 模型的字段定义上，GORM 默认将 `string` 类型映射为 `longtext`，但我们需要为 email 字段指定合适的长度。

修改 `internal/model/user.go` 文件：

```go
package model

import (
    "time"
    "gorm.io/gorm"
)

type User struct {
    ID        uint           `json:"id" gorm:"primarykey"`
    Name      string         `json:"name" gorm:"type:varchar(100);not null"`
    Email     string         `json:"email" gorm:"type:varchar(191);uniqueIndex;not null"`
    CreatedAt time.Time      `json:"created_at"`
    UpdatedAt time.Time      `json:"updated_at"`
    DeletedAt gorm.DeletedAt `json:"-" gorm:"index"`
}

type CreateUserRequest struct {
    Name  string `json:"name" binding:"required"`
    Email string `json:"email" binding:"required,email"`
}

type UpdateUserRequest struct {
    Name  string `json:"name"`
    Email string `json:"email" binding:"omitempty,email"`
}
```

主要修改：


1. **Name 字段**：`gorm:"type:varchar(100);not null"` - 指定为 varchar(100) 类型
2. **Email 字段**：`gorm:"type:varchar(191);uniqueIndex;not null"` - 指定为 varchar(191) 类型

为什么选择 191 长度？在 MySQL 中使用 utf8mb4 字符集时，每个字符最多占用 4 字节，而 MySQL 的索引键长度限制是 767 字节（InnoDB 引擎默认），所以 767 ÷ 4 = 191.75，取整为 191 是安全的长度。

如果你需要支持更长的邮箱地址，也可以使用以下替代方案：

**方案一：使用更大的长度（需要 MySQL 5.7+ 且开启 innodb_large_prefix）**

```go
type User struct {
    ID        uint           `json:"id" gorm:"primarykey"`
    Name      string         `json:"name" gorm:"type:varchar(255);not null"`
    Email     string         `json:"email" gorm:"type:varchar(255);uniqueIndex;not null"`
    CreatedAt time.Time      `json:"created_at"`
    UpdatedAt time.Time      `json:"updated_at"`
    DeletedAt gorm.DeletedAt `json:"-" gorm:"index"`
}
```

**方案二：使用 text 类型但指定索引长度**

```go
type User struct {
    ID        uint           `json:"id" gorm:"primarykey"`
    Name      string         `json:"name" gorm:"type:text;not null"`
    Email     string         `json:"email" gorm:"type:text;uniqueIndex:idx_users_email,length:191;not null"`
    CreatedAt time.Time      `json:"created_at"`
    UpdatedAt time.Time      `json:"updated_at"`
    DeletedAt gorm.DeletedAt `json:"-" gorm:"index"`
}
```

**方案三：在数据库配置中优化（推荐）**

同时，你可以在 `pkg/database/database.go` 中添加一些数据库配置优化：

```go
package database

import (
    "fmt"
    "your-project/internal/config"
    "your-project/internal/model"
    
    "gorm.io/driver/mysql"
    "gorm.io/gorm"
    "gorm.io/gorm/logger"
)

func NewDatabase(cfg *config.Config) (*gorm.DB, error) {
    dsn := fmt.Sprintf("%s:%s@tcp(%s:%s)/%s?charset=utf8mb4&parseTime=True&loc=Local&innodb_large_prefix=1",
        cfg.Database.User,
        cfg.Database.Password,
        cfg.Database.Host,
        cfg.Database.Port,
        cfg.Database.DBName,
    )
    
    db, err := gorm.Open(mysql.Open(dsn), &gorm.Config{
        Logger: logger.Default.LogMode(logger.Info),
        NamingStrategy: schema.NamingStrategy{
            SingularTable: true, // 使用单数表名
        },
    })
    if err != nil {
        return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to connect database: %w", err)
    }
    
    // 设置连接池
    sqlDB, err := db.DB()
    if err != nil {
        return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get underlying sql.DB: %w", err)
    }
    
    sqlDB.SetMaxIdleConns(10)
    sqlDB.SetMaxOpenConns(100)
    sqlDB.SetConnMaxLifetime(time.Hour)
    
    // 自动迁移
    if err := db.AutoMigrate(&model.User{}); err != nil {
        return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to migrate database: %w", err)
    }
    
    return db, nil
}
```

记得在文件顶部添加必要的导入：

```go
import (
    "fmt"
    "time"
    "your-project/internal/config"
    "your-project/internal/model"
    
    "gorm.io/driver/mysql"
    "gorm.io/gorm"
    "gorm.io/gorm/logger"
    "gorm.io/gorm/schema"
)
```

使用第一个方案（varchar(191)）是最简单和兼容性最好的解决方案。修改后重新运行程序即可解决这个问题。

#### USER

在main.go如何读取应用配置呢？

#### ASSISTANT

在当前的架构中，配置读取是通过 Wire 依赖注入自动处理的，但如果你需要在 `main.go` 中直接访问配置（比如设置服务器端口、运行模式等），有几种方式可以实现：

### 方案一：修改 Wire 注入，返回配置对象

**修改 wire/wire.go**

```go
//go:build wireinject
// +build wireinject

package wire

import (
    "your-project/internal/api/handler"
    "your-project/internal/api/router"
    "your-project/internal/config"
    "your-project/internal/repository"
    "your-project/internal/service"
    "your-project/pkg/database"
    
    "github.com/gin-gonic/gin"
    "github.com/google/wire"
)

// 定义应用结构体
type App struct {
    Router *gin.Engine
    Config *config.Config
}

func InitializeApp() (*App, error) {
    wire.Build(
        config.LoadConfig,
        database.NewDatabase,
        repository.NewUserRepository,
        service.NewUserService,
        handler.NewUserHandler,
        router.SetupRouter,
        wire.Struct(new(App), "*"), // 创建 App 结构体
    )
    return &App{}, nil
}
```

**修改 cmd/server/main.go**

```go
package main

import (
    "fmt"
    "log"
    "your-project/wire"
    
    "github.com/gin-gonic/gin"
)

func main() {
    app, err := wire.InitializeApp()
    if err != nil {
        log.Fatal("Failed to initialize app:", err)
    }
    
    // 设置 Gin 运行模式
    gin.SetMode(app.Config.Server.Mode)
    
    // 使用配置中的端口
    addr := fmt.Sprintf(":%s", app.Config.Server.Port)
    log.Printf("Server starting on %s in %s mode", addr, app.Config.Server.Mode)
    
    if err := app.Router.Run(addr); err != nil {
        log.Fatal("Failed to start server:", err)
    }
}
```

### 方案二：在 main.go 中直接读取配置

**修改 cmd/server/main.go**

```go
package main

import (
    "fmt"
    "log"
    "your-project/internal/config"
    "your-project/wire"
    
    "github.com/gin-gonic/gin"
)

func main() {
    // 先读取配置
    cfg, err := config.LoadConfig()
    if err != nil {
        log.Fatal("Failed to load config:", err)
    }
    
    // 设置 Gin 运行模式
    gin.SetMode(cfg.Server.Mode)
    
    // 初始化应用
    router, err := wire.InitializeApp()
    if err != nil {
        log.Fatal("Failed to initialize app:", err)
    }
    
    // 使用配置中的端口启动服务器
    addr := fmt.Sprintf(":%s", cfg.Server.Port)
    log.Printf("Server starting on %s in %s mode", addr, cfg.Server.Mode)
    
    if err := router.Run(addr); err != nil {
        log.Fatal("Failed to start server:", err)
    }
}
```

但这种方式会导致配置被读取两次，不够优雅。

### 方案三：增强配置结构，添加更多配置项

**扩展 internal/config/config.go**

```go
package config

import (
    "log"
    "os"
    
    "github.com/spf13/viper"
)

type Config struct {
    Server   ServerConfig   `mapstructure:"server"`
    Database DatabaseConfig `mapstructure:"database"`
    Log      LogConfig      `mapstructure:"log"`
    App      AppConfig      `mapstructure:"app"`
}

type ServerConfig struct {
    Port         string `mapstructure:"port"`
    Mode         string `mapstructure:"mode"`
    ReadTimeout  int    `mapstructure:"read_timeout"`
    WriteTimeout int    `mapstructure:"write_timeout"`
}

type DatabaseConfig struct {
    Host         string `mapstructure:"host"`
    Port         string `mapstructure:"port"`
    User         string `mapstructure:"user"`
    Password     string `mapstructure:"password"`
    DBName       string `mapstructure:"dbname"`
    MaxIdleConns int    `mapstructure:"max_idle_conns"`
    MaxOpenConns int    `mapstructure:"max_open_conns"`
}

type LogConfig struct {
    Level  string `mapstructure:"level"`
    Format string `mapstructure:"format"`
}

type AppConfig struct {
    Name    string `mapstructure:"name"`
    Version string `mapstructure:"version"`
    Debug   bool   `mapstructure:"debug"`
}

func LoadConfig() (*Config, error) {
    viper.SetConfigName("config")
    viper.SetConfigType("yaml")
    viper.AddConfigPath(".")
    viper.AddConfigPath("./configs")
    viper.AddConfigPath("/etc/app/")
    
    // 支持环境变量
    viper.AutomaticEnv()
    viper.SetEnvPrefix("APP")
    
    // 设置默认值
    setDefaults()
    
    // 尝试读取配置文件
    if err := viper.ReadInConfig(); err != nil {
        if _, ok := err.(viper.ConfigFileNotFoundError); ok {
            log.Println("Config file not found, using defaults and environment variables")
        } else {
            return nil, err
        }
    } else {
        log.Printf("Using config file: %s", viper.ConfigFileUsed())
    }
    
    var config Config
    if err := viper.Unmarshal(&config); err != nil {
        return nil, err
    }
    
    return &config, nil
}

func setDefaults() {
    // Server defaults
    viper.SetDefault("server.port", "8080")
    viper.SetDefault("server.mode", "debug")
    viper.SetDefault("server.read_timeout", 30)
    viper.SetDefault("server.write_timeout", 30)
    
    // Database defaults
    viper.SetDefault("database.host", "localhost")
    viper.SetDefault("database.port", "3306")
    viper.SetDefault("database.max_idle_conns", 10)
    viper.SetDefault("database.max_open_conns", 100)
    
    // Log defaults
    viper.SetDefault("log.level", "info")
    viper.SetDefault("log.format", "json")
    
    // App defaults
    viper.SetDefault("app.name", "wire-demo")
    viper.SetDefault("app.version", "1.0.0")
    viper.SetDefault("app.debug", false)
}

// GetEnv 获取环境变量，如果不存在则返回默认值
func GetEnv(key, defaultValue string) string {
    if value := os.Getenv(key); value != "" {
        return value
    }
    return defaultValue
}
```

**对应的配置文件 config.yaml**

```yaml
server:
  port: "8080"
  mode: "debug"  # debug, release, test
  read_timeout: 30
  write_timeout: 30

database:
  host: "localhost"
  port: "3306"
  user: "root"
  password: "password"
  dbname: "wire_demo"
  max_idle_conns: 10
  max_open_conns: 100

log:
  level: "info"  # debug, info, warn, error
  format: "json" # json, text

app:
  name: "wire-demo"
  version: "1.0.0"
  debug: true
```

**使用方案一的最终 main.go**

```go
package main

import (
    "context"
    "fmt"
    "log"
    "net/http"
    "os"
    "os/signal"
    "syscall"
    "time"
    "your-project/wire"
    
    "github.com/gin-gonic/gin"
)

func main() {
    app, err := wire.InitializeApp()
    if err != nil {
        log.Fatal("Failed to initialize app:", err)
    }
    
    // 设置 Gin 运行模式
    gin.SetMode(app.Config.Server.Mode)
    
    // 创建 HTTP 服务器
    addr := fmt.Sprintf(":%s", app.Config.Server.Port)
    srv := &http.Server{
        Addr:         addr,
        Handler:      app.Router,
        ReadTimeout:  time.Duration(app.Config.Server.ReadTimeout) * time.Second,
        WriteTimeout: time.Duration(app.Config.Server.WriteTimeout) * time.Second,
    }
    
    // 在 goroutine 中启动服务器
    go func() {
        log.Printf("Server starting on %s in %s mode", addr, app.Config.Server.Mode)
        log.Printf("App: %s v%s", app.Config.App.Name, app.Config.App.Version)
        
        if err := srv.ListenAndServe(); err != nil && err != http.ErrServerClosed {
            log.Fatalf("Failed to start server: %v", err)
        }
    }()
    
    // 优雅关闭
    gracefulShutdown(srv)
}

func gracefulShutdown(srv *http.Server) {
    quit := make(chan os.Signal, 1)
    signal.Notify(quit, syscall.SIGINT, syscall.SIGTERM)
    <-quit
    
    log.Println("Shutting down server...")
    
    ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 30*time.Second)
    defer cancel()
    
    if err := srv.Shutdown(ctx); err != nil {
        log.Fatal("Server forced to shutdown:", err)
    }
    
    log.Println("Server exited")
}
```

推荐使用**方案一**，它通过 Wire 依赖注入统一管理配置，避免重复读取，同时在 main.go 中可以方便地访问所有配置项。这样既保持了架构的一致性，又提供了足够的灵活性。

\

#### ASSISTANT

你的理解是对的！让我详细解释一下 Wire 的工作机制：

### Wire 工作原理


1. **wire.go 文件**：这是你写的"模板"文件，告诉 Wire 如何组装依赖
2. **wire_gen.go 文件**：这是 Wire 工具自动生成的实际代码，包含真正的依赖注入逻辑
3. **实际运行时**：程序调用的是 `wire_gen.go` 中的代码

### 具体流程

**第一步：你写 wire.go（模板）**

```go
//go:build wireinject
// +build wireinject

package wire

import (
    "wire-demo/internal/config"
    // ... 其他导入
    "github.com/google/wire"
)

type App struct {
    Engine *gin.Engine  // 注意这里应该是指针
    Config *config.Config
}

func InitializeApp() (*App, error) {
    wire.Build(
        config.LoadConfig,
        database.NewDatabase,
        repository.NewUserRepository,
        service.NewUserService,
        api.NewUserHandler,
        router.SetupRouter,
        wire.Struct(new(App), "*"),
    )
    return &App{}, nil  // 这行代码不会执行，只是占位
}
```

**第二步：运行 wire 命令生成代码**

```bash
cd wire
wire
```

**第三步：Wire 生成 wire_gen.go（实际代码）**

```go
// Code generated by Wire. DO NOT EDIT.

//go:generate go run github.com/google/wire/cmd/wire
//go:build !wireinject
// +build !wireinject

package wire

import (
    "wire-demo/internal/api"
    "wire-demo/internal/config"
    "wire-demo/internal/repository"
    "wire-demo/internal/router"
    "wire-demo/internal/service"
    "wire-demo/pkg/database"
)

// Injectors from wire.go:

func InitializeApp() (*App, error) {
    configConfig, err := config.LoadConfig()
    if err != nil {
        return nil, err
    }
    db, err := database.NewDatabase(configConfig)
    if err != nil {
        return nil, err
    }
    userRepository := repository.NewUserRepository(db)
    userService := service.NewUserService(userRepository)
    userHandler := api.NewUserHandler(userService)
    engine := router.SetupRouter(userHandler)
    app := &App{
        Engine: engine,
        Config: configConfig,
    }
    return app, nil
}
```

### 关键点说明


1. **构建标签**：
   * `//go:build wireinject` 表示这个文件只在 wire 生成时使用
   * `//go:build !wireinject` 表示这个文件在正常编译时使用
2. **实际调用**：

   ```go
   // main.go 中调用的实际是 wire_gen.go 中的函数
   app, err := wire.InitializeApp()  // 调用生成的代码
   ```
3. **你的 wire.go 有个小问题**：

   ```go
   type App struct {
       Engine *gin.Engine     // 应该是指针
       Config *config.Config  // 应该是指针
   }
   ```

### 完整的正确示例

**wire/wire.go**

```go
//go:build wireinject
// +build wireinject

package wire

import (
    "wire-demo/internal/api"
    "wire-demo/internal/config"
    "wire-demo/internal/repository"
    "wire-demo/internal/router"
    "wire-demo/internal/service"
    "wire-demo/pkg/database"
    
    "github.com/gin-gonic/gin"
    "github.com/google/wire"
)

type App struct {
    Engine *gin.Engine
    Config *config.Config
}

func InitializeApp() (*App, error) {
    wire.Build(
        config.LoadConfig,
        database.NewDatabase,
        repository.NewUserRepository,
        service.NewUserService,
        api.NewUserHandler,
        router.SetupRouter,
        wire.Struct(new(App), "*"),
    )
    return &App{}, nil
}
```

**生成命令**

```bash
cd wire
wire
```

**main.go**

```go
package main

import (
    "fmt"
    "log"
    "wire-demo/wire"  // 导入 wire 包
    
    "github.com/gin-gonic/gin"
)

func main() {
    // 实际调用的是 wire_gen.go 中的 InitializeApp
    app, err := wire.InitializeApp()
    if err != nil {
        log.Fatal("Failed to initialize app:", err)
    }
    
    gin.SetMode(app.Config.Server.Mode)
    
    addr := fmt.Sprintf(":%s", app.Config.Server.Port)
    log.Printf("Server starting on %s", addr)
    
    if err := app.Engine.Run(addr); err != nil {
        log.Fatal("Failed to start server:", err)
    }
}
```

### 总结

* **wire.go**：你写的模板，告诉 Wire 怎么组装
* **wire_gen.go**：Wire 自动生成的实际代码
* **运行时**：程序执行的是 `wire_gen.go` 中的代码
* **每次修改 wire.go 后都需要重新运行** `wire` 命令

这就是为什么 Wire 被称为"编译时依赖注入"框架，因为依赖关系在编译前就已经确定并生成了具体的代码。

**internal/model/user.go**

```go
package model

import (
    "time"
    "gorm.io/gorm"
)

type User struct {
    ID        uint           `json:"id" gorm:"primarykey"`
    Name      string         `json:"name" gorm:"type:varchar(100);not null"`
    Email     string         `json:"email" gorm:"type:varchar(191);uniqueIndex;not null"`
    CreatedAt time.Time      `json:"created_at"`
    UpdatedAt time.Time      `json:"updated_at"`
    DeletedAt gorm.DeletedAt `json:"-" gorm:"index"`
}

type CreateUserRequest struct {
    Name  string `json:"name" binding:"required"`
    Email string `json:"email" binding:"required,email"`
}

type UpdateUserRequest struct {
    Name  string `json:"name"`
    Email string `json:"email" binding:"omitempty,email"`
}

type ListUsersRequest struct {
    Page     int `form:"page" binding:"min=1"`
    PageSize int `form:"page_size" binding:"min=1,max=100"`
}

type ListUsersResponse struct {
    Users []*User `json:"users"`
    Total int64   `json:"total"`
    Page  int     `json:"page"`
    Size  int     `json:"size"`
}
```

**internal/repository/user_repository.go**

```go
package repository

import (
    "wire-demo/internal/model"
    "gorm.io/gorm"
)

//go:generate mockgen -source=user_repository.go -destination=../../mocks/mock_user_repository.go

type UserRepository interface {
    Create(user *model.User) error
    GetByID(id uint) (*model.User, error)
    GetByEmail(email string) (*model.User, error)
    Update(user *model.User) error
    Delete(id uint) error
    List(offset, limit int) ([]*model.User, int64, error)
    ExistsByEmail(email string) (bool, error)
}

type userRepository struct {
    db *gorm.DB
}

func NewUserRepository(db *gorm.DB) UserRepository {
    return &userRepository{db: db}
}

func (r *userRepository) Create(user *model.User) error {
    return r.db.Create(user).Error
}

func (r *userRepository) GetByID(id uint) (*model.User, error) {
    var user model.User
    err := r.db.First(&user, id).Error
    if err != nil {
        return nil, err
    }
    return &user, nil
}

func (r *userRepository) GetByEmail(email string) (*model.User, error) {
    var user model.User
    err := r.db.Where("email = ?", email).First(&user).Error
    if err != nil {
        return nil, err
    }
    return &user, nil
}

func (r *userRepository) Update(user *model.User) error {
    return r.db.Save(user).Error
}

func (r *userRepository) Delete(id uint) error {
    return r.db.Delete(&model.User{}, id).Error
}

func (r *userRepository) List(offset, limit int) ([]*model.User, int64, error) {
    var users []*model.User
    var total int64
    
    if err := r.db.Model(&model.User{}).Count(&total).Error; err != nil {
        return nil, 0, err
    }
    
    err := r.db.Offset(offset).Limit(limit).Find(&users).Error
    return users, total, err
}

func (r *userRepository) ExistsByEmail(email string) (bool, error) {
    var count int64
    err := r.db.Model(&model.User{}).Where("email = ?", email).Count(&count).Error
    return count > 0, err
}
```

**internal/repository/wire.go**

```go
package repository

import "github.com/google/wire"

// RepositorySet 仓储层依赖注入集合
var RepositorySet = wire.NewSet(
    NewUserRepository,
    wire.Bind(new(UserRepository), new(*userRepository)),
)
```

**internal/repository/user_repository_test.go**

```go
package repository

import (
    "testing"
    "wire-demo/internal/model"
    
    "github.com/stretchr/testify/assert"
    "github.com/stretchr/testify/suite"
    "gorm.io/driver/sqlite"
    "gorm.io/gorm"
)

type UserRepositoryTestSuite struct {
    suite.Suite
    db   *gorm.DB
    repo UserRepository
}

func (suite *UserRepositoryTestSuite) SetupSuite() {
    db, err := gorm.Open(sqlite.Open(":memory:"), &gorm.Config{})
    suite.Require().NoError(err)
    
    err = db.AutoMigrate(&model.User{})
    suite.Require().NoError(err)
    
    suite.db = db
    suite.repo = NewUserRepository(db)
}

func (suite *UserRepositoryTestSuite) SetupTest() {
    // 清理数据
    suite.db.Exec("DELETE FROM users")
}

func (suite *UserRepositoryTestSuite) TestCreate() {
    user := &model.User{
        Name:  "Test User",
        Email: "test@example.com",
    }
    
    err := suite.repo.Create(user)
    assert.NoError(suite.T(), err)
    assert.NotZero(suite.T(), user.ID)
}

func (suite *UserRepositoryTestSuite) TestGetByID() {
    // 创建用户
    user := &model.User{
        Name:  "Test User",
        Email: "test@example.com",
    }
    err := suite.repo.Create(user)
    suite.Require().NoError(err)
    
    // 获取用户
    foundUser, err := suite.repo.GetByID(user.ID)
    assert.NoError(suite.T(), err)
    assert.Equal(suite.T(), user.Name, foundUser.Name)
    assert.Equal(suite.T(), user.Email, foundUser.Email)
}

func (suite *UserRepositoryTestSuite) TestGetByEmail() {
    user := &model.User{
        Name:  "Test User",
        Email: "test@example.com",
    }
    err := suite.repo.Create(user)
    suite.Require().NoError(err)
    
    foundUser, err := suite.repo.GetByEmail(user.Email)
    assert.NoError(suite.T(), err)
    assert.Equal(suite.T(), user.ID, foundUser.ID)
}

func (suite *UserRepositoryTestSuite) TestUpdate() {
    user := &model.User{
        Name:  "Test User",
        Email: "test@example.com",
    }
    err := suite.repo.Create(user)
    suite.Require().NoError(err)
    
    user.Name = "Updated User"
    err = suite.repo.Update(user)
    assert.NoError(suite.T(), err)
    
    updatedUser, err := suite.repo.GetByID(user.ID)
    assert.NoError(suite.T(), err)
    assert.Equal(suite.T(), "Updated User", updatedUser.Name)
}

func (suite *UserRepositoryTestSuite) TestDelete() {
    user := &model.User{
        Name:  "Test User",
        Email: "test@example.com",
    }
    err := suite.repo.Create(user)
    suite.Require().NoError(err)
    
    err = suite.repo.Delete(user.ID)
    assert.NoError(suite.T(), err)
    
    _, err = suite.repo.GetByID(user.ID)
    assert.Error(suite.T(), err)
}

func (suite *UserRepositoryTestSuite) TestList() {
    // 创建多个用户
    users := []*model.User{
        {Name: "User1", Email: "user1@example.com"},
        {Name: "User2", Email: "user2@example.com"},
        {Name: "User3", Email: "user3@example.com"},
    }
    
    for _, user := range users {
        err := suite.repo.Create(user)
        suite.Require().NoError(err)
    }
    
    foundUsers, total, err := suite.repo.List(0, 2)
    assert.NoError(suite.T(), err)
    assert.Equal(suite.T(), int64(3), total)
    assert.Len(suite.T(), foundUsers, 2)
}

func (suite *UserRepositoryTestSuite) TestExistsByEmail() {
    user := &model.User{
        Name:  "Test User",
        Email: "test@example.com",
    }
    err := suite.repo.Create(user)
    suite.Require().NoError(err)
    
    exists, err := suite.repo.ExistsByEmail("test@example.com")
    assert.NoError(suite.T(), err)
    assert.True(suite.T(), exists)
    
    exists, err = suite.repo.ExistsByEmail("nonexistent@example.com")
    assert.NoError(suite.T(), err)
    assert.False(suite.T(), exists)
}

func TestUserRepositoryTestSuite(t *testing.T) {
    suite.Run(t, new(UserRepositoryTestSuite))
}
```

**internal/service/user_service.go**

```go
package service

import (
    "errors"
    "wire-demo/internal/model"
    "wire-demo/internal/repository"
    "gorm.io/gorm"
)

//go:generate mockgen -source=user_service.go -destination=../../mocks/mock_user_service.go

type UserService interface {
    CreateUser(req *model.CreateUserRequest) (*model.User, error)
    GetUser(id uint) (*model.User, error)
    UpdateUser(id uint, req *model.UpdateUserRequest) (*model.User, error)
    DeleteUser(id uint) error
    ListUsers(req *model.ListUsersRequest) (*model.ListUsersResponse, error)
}

type userService struct {
    userRepo repository.UserRepository
}

func NewUserService(userRepo repository.UserRepository) UserService {
    return &userService{
        userRepo: userRepo,
    }
}

func (s *userService) CreateUser(req *model.CreateUserRequest) (*model.User, error) {
    // 检查邮箱是否已存在
    exists, err := s.userRepo.ExistsByEmail(req.Email)
    if err != nil {
        return nil, err
    }
    if exists {
        return nil, errors.New("email already exists")
    }
    
    user := &model.User{
        Name:  req.Name,
        Email: req.Email,
    }
    
    if err := s.userRepo.Create(user); err != nil {
        return nil, err
    }
    
    return user, nil
}

func (s *userService) GetUser(id uint) (*model.User, error) {
    return s.userRepo.GetByID(id)
}

func (s *userService) UpdateUser(id uint, req *model.UpdateUserRequest) (*model.User, error) {
    user, err := s.userRepo.GetByID(id)
    if err != nil {
        if errors.Is(err, gorm.ErrRecordNotFound) {
            return nil, errors.New("user not found")
        }
        return nil, err
    }
    
    if req.Name != "" {
        user.Name = req.Name
    }
    if req.Email != "" {
        // 检查新邮箱是否已被其他用户使用
        if existingUser, err := s.userRepo.GetByEmail(req.Email); err == nil && existingUser.ID != id {
            return nil, errors.New("email already exists")
        }
        user.Email = req.Email
    }
    
    if err := s.userRepo.Update(user); err != nil {
        return nil, err
    }
    
    return user, nil
}

func (s *userService) DeleteUser(id uint) error {
    if _, err := s.userRepo.GetByID(id); err != nil {
        if errors.Is(err, gorm.ErrRecordNotFound) {
            return errors.New("user not found")
        }
        return err
    }
    
    return s.userRepo.Delete(id)
}

func (s *userService) ListUsers(req *model.ListUsersRequest) (*model.ListUsersResponse, error) {
    offset := (req.Page - 1) * req.PageSize
    users, total, err := s.userRepo.List(offset, req.PageSize)
    if err != nil {
        return nil, err
    }
    
    return &model.ListUsersResponse{
        Users: users,
        Total: total,
        Page:  req.Page,
        Size:  req.PageSize,
    }, nil
}
```

```go
package service

import (
    "errors"
    "testing"
    "wire-demo/internal/model"
    "wire-demo/mocks"
    
    "github.com/golang/mock/gomock"
    "github.com/stretchr/testify/assert"
    "gorm.io/gorm"
)

func TestUserService_CreateUser(t *testing.T) {
    ctrl := gomock.NewController(t)
    defer ctrl.Finish()
    
    mockRepo := mocks.NewMockUserRepository(ctrl)
    service := NewUserService(mockRepo)
    
    t.Run("success", func(t *testing.T) {
        req := &model.CreateUserRequest{
            Name:  "Test User",
            Email: "test@example.com",
        }
        
        mockRepo.EXPECT().
            ExistsByEmail(req.Email).
            Return(false, nil)
        
        mockRepo.EXPECT().
            Create(gomock.Any()).
            DoAndReturn(func(user *model.User) error {
                user.ID = 1
                return nil
            })
        
        user, err := service.CreateUser(req)
        assert.NoError(t, err)
        assert.Equal(t, req.Name, user.Name)
        assert.Equal(t, req.Email, user.Email)
        assert.Equal(t, uint(1), user.ID)
    })
    
    t.Run("email already exists", func(t *testing.T) {
        req := &model.CreateUserRequest{
            Name:  "Test User",
            Email: "test@example.com",
        }
        
        mockRepo.EXPECT().
            ExistsByEmail(req.Email).
            Return(true, nil)
        
        user, err := service.CreateUser(req)
        assert.Error(t, err)
        assert.Nil(t, user)
        assert.Contains(t, err.Error(), "email already exists")
    })
}

func TestUserService_GetUser(t *testing.T) {
    ctrl := gomock.NewController(t)
    defer ctrl.Finish()
    
    mockRepo := mocks.NewMockUserRepository(ctrl)
    service := NewUserService(mockRepo)
    
    t.Run("success", func(t *testing.T) {
        expectedUser := &model.User{
            ID:    1,
            Name:  "Test User",
            Email: "test@example.com",
        }
        
        mockRepo.EXPECT().
            GetByID(uint(1)).
            Return(expectedUser, nil)
        
        user, err := service.GetUser(1)
        assert.NoError(t, err)
        assert.Equal(t, expectedUser, user)
    })
    
    t.Run("user not found", func(t *testing.T) {
        mockRepo.EXPECT().
            GetByID(uint(999)).
            Return(nil, gorm.ErrRecordNotFound)
        
        user, err := service.GetUser(999)
        assert.Error(t, err)
        assert.Nil(t, user)
    })
}

        assert.Equal(t, req.Email, user.Email)

        assert.Equal(t, 10, response.Size)

        req := &model.ListUsersRequest{
            Page:     1,
            PageSize: 10,
        }
        
        mockRepo.EXPECT().
            List(0, 10).
            Return(nil, int64(0), errors.New("database error"))
        
        response, err := service.ListUsers(req)
        assert.Error(t, err)
        assert.Nil(t, response)
    })
}
```

**internal/api/handler/user_handler.go**

```go
package handler

import (
    "net/http"
    "strconv"
    "wire-demo/internal/model"
    "wire-demo/internal/service"
    
    "github.com/gin-gonic/gin"
)

type UserHandler struct {
    userService service.UserService
}

func NewUserHandler(userService service.UserService) *UserHandler {
    return &UserHandler{
        userService: userService,
    }
}

func (h *UserHandler) CreateUser(c *gin.Context) {
    var req model.CreateUserRequest
    if err := c.ShouldBindJSON(&req); err != nil {
        c.JSON(http.StatusBadRequest, gin.H{"error": err.Error()})
        return
    }
    
    user, err := h.userService.CreateUser(&req)
    if err != nil {
        c.JSON(http.StatusBadRequest, gin.H{"error": err.Error()})
        return
    }
    
    c.JSON(http.StatusCreated, gin.H{"data": user})
}

func (h *UserHandler) GetUser(c *gin.Context) {
    idStr := c.Param("id")
    id, err := strconv.ParseUint(idStr, 10, 32)
    if err != nil {
        c.JSON(http.StatusBadRequest, gin.H{"error": "invalid user id"})
        return
    }
    
    user, err := h.userService.GetUser(uint(id))
    if err != nil {
        c.JSON(http.StatusNotFound, gin.H{"error": "user not found"})
        return
    }
    
    c.JSON(http.StatusOK, gin.H{"data": user})
}

func (h *UserHandler) UpdateUser(c *gin.Context) {
    idStr := c.Param("id")
    id, err := strconv.ParseUint(idStr, 10, 32)
    if err != nil {
        c.JSON(http.StatusBadRequest, gin.H{"error": "invalid user id"})
        return
    }
    
    var req model.UpdateUserRequest
    if err := c.ShouldBindJSON(&req); err != nil {
        c.JSON(http.StatusBadRequest, gin.H{"error": err.Error()})
        return
    }
    
    user, err := h.userService.UpdateUser(uint(id), &req)
    if err != nil {
        c.JSON(http.StatusBadRequest, gin.H{"error": err.Error()})
        return
    }
    
    c.JSON(http.StatusOK, gin.H{"data": user})
}

func (h *UserHandler) DeleteUser(c *gin.Context) {
    idStr := c.Param("id")
    id, err := strconv.ParseUint(idStr, 10, 32)
    if err != nil {
        c.JSON(http.StatusBadRequest, gin.H{"error": "invalid user id"})
        return
    }
    
    if err := h.userService.DeleteUser(uint(id)); err != nil {
        c.JSON(http.StatusBadRequest, gin.H{"error": err.Error()})
        return
    }
    
    c.JSON(http.StatusOK, gin.H{"message": "user deleted successfully"})
}

func (h *UserHandler) ListUsers(c *gin.Context) {
    var req model.ListUsersRequest
    if err := c.ShouldBindQuery(&req); err != nil {
        c.JSON(http.StatusBadRequest, gin.H{"error": err.Error()})
        return
    }
    
    // 设置默认值
    if req.Page == 0 {
        req.Page = 1
    }
    if req.PageSize == 0 {
        req.PageSize = 10
    }
    
    response, err := h.userService.ListUsers(&req)
    if err != nil {
        c.JSON(http.StatusInternalServerError, gin.H{"error": err.Error()})
        return
    }
    
    c.JSON(http.StatusOK, gin.H{"data": response})
}
```

**internal/api/handler/user_handler_test.go**

```go
package handler

import (
    "bytes"
    "encoding/json"
    "net/http"
    "net/http/httptest"
    "testing"
    "wire-demo/internal/model"
    "wire-demo/mocks"
    
    "github.com/gin-gonic/gin"
    "github.com/golang/mock/gomock"
    "github.com/stretchr/testify/assert"
)

func setupTestRouter(handler *UserHandler) *gin.Engine {
    gin.SetMode(gin.TestMode)
    r := gin.New()
    
    api := r.Group("/api/v1")
    users := api.Group("/users")
    {
        users.POST("", handler.CreateUser)
        users.GET("/:id", handler.GetUser)
        users.PUT("/:id", handler.UpdateUser)
        users.DELETE("/:id", handler.DeleteUser)
        users.GET("", handler.ListUsers)
    }
    
    return r
}

func TestUserHandler_CreateUser(t *testing.T) {
    ctrl := gomock.NewController(t)
    defer ctrl.Finish()
    
    mockService := mocks.NewMockUserService(ctrl)
    handler := NewUserHandler(mockService)
    router := setupTestRouter(handler)
    
    t.Run("success", func(t *testing.T) {
        req := model.CreateUserRequest{
            Name:  "Test User",
            Email: "test@example.com",
        }
        
        expectedUser := &model.User{
            ID:    1,
            Name:  req.Name,
            Email: req.Email,
        }
        
        mockService.EXPECT().
            CreateUser(&req).
            Return(expectedUser, nil)
        
        reqBody, _ := json.Marshal(req)
        w := httptest.NewRecorder()
        httpReq, _ := http.NewRequest("POST", "/api/v1/users", bytes.NewBuffer(reqBody))
        httpReq.Header.Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
        
        router.ServeHTTP(w, httpReq)
        
        assert.Equal(t, http.StatusCreated, w.Code)
        
        var response map[string]interface{}
        err := json.Unmarshal(w.Body.Bytes(), &response)
        assert.NoError(t, err)
        
        data := response["data"].(map[string]interface{})
        assert.Equal(t, float64(1), data["id"])
        assert.Equal(t, req.Name, data["name"])
        assert.Equal(t, req.Email, data["email"])
    })
    
    t.Run("invalid request", func(t *testing.T) {
        reqBody := []byte(`{"name": ""}`) // missing email
        w := httptest.NewRecorder()
        httpReq, _ := http.NewRequest("POST", "/api/v1/users", bytes.NewBuffer(reqBody))
        httpReq.Header.Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
        
        router.ServeHTTP(w, httpReq)
        
        assert.Equal(t, http.StatusBadRequest, w.Code)
    })
}

func TestUserHandler_GetUser(t *testing.T) {
    ctrl := gomock.NewController(t)
    defer ctrl.Finish()
    
    mockService := mocks.NewMockUserService(ctrl)
    handler := NewUserHandler(mockService)
    router := setupTestRouter(handler)
    
    t.Run("success", func(t *testing.T) {
        expectedUser := &model.User{
            ID:    1,
            Name:  "Test User",
            Email: "test@example.com",
        }
        
        mockService.EXPECT().
            GetUser(uint(1)).
            Return(expectedUser, nil)
        
        w := httptest.NewRecorder()
        httpReq, _ := http.NewRequest("GET", "/api/v1/users/1", nil)
        
        router.ServeHTTP(w, httpReq)
        
        assert.Equal(t, http.StatusOK, w.Code)
        
        var response map[string]interface{}
        err := json.Unmarshal(w.Body.Bytes(), &response)
        assert.NoError(t, err)
        
        data := response["data"].(map[string]interface{})
        assert.Equal(t, float64(1), data["id"])
        assert.Equal(t, expectedUser.Name, data["name"])
        assert.Equal(t, expectedUser.Email, data["email"])
    })
    
    t.Run("invalid id", func(t *testing.T) {
        w := httptest.NewRecorder()
        httpReq, _ := http.NewRequest("GET", "/api/v1/users/invalid", nil)
        
        router.ServeHTTP(w, httpReq)
        
        assert.Equal(t, http.StatusBadRequest, w.Code)
    })
}

func TestUserHandler_ListUsers(t *testing.T) {
    ctrl := gomock.NewController(t)
    defer ctrl.Finish()
    
    mockService := mocks.NewMockUserService(ctrl)
    handler := NewUserHandler(mockService)
    router := setupTestRouter(handler)
    
    t.Run("success", func(t *testing.T) {
        expectedResponse := &model.ListUsersResponse{
            Users: []*model.User{
                {ID: 1, Name: "User1", Email: "user1@example.com"},
                {ID: 2, Name: "User2", Email: "user2@example.com"},
            },
            Total: 2,
            Page:  1,
            Size:  10,
        }
        
        mockService.EXPECT().
            ListUsers(gomock.Any()).
            DoAndReturn(func(req *model.ListUsersRequest) (*model.ListUsersResponse, error) {
                assert.Equal(t, 1, req.Page)
                assert.Equal(t, 10, req.PageSize)
                return expectedResponse, nil
            })
        
        w := httptest.NewRecorder()
        httpReq, _ := http.NewRequest("GET", "/api/v1/users", nil)
        
        router.ServeHTTP(w, httpReq)
        
        assert.Equal(t, http.StatusOK, w.Code)
        
        var response map[string]interface{}
        err := json.Unmarshal(w.Body.Bytes(), &response)
        assert.NoError(t, err)
        
        data := response["data"].(map[string]interface{})
        assert.Equal(t, float64(2), data["total"])
        assert.Equal(t, float64(1), data["page"])
        assert.Equal(t, float64(10), data["size"])
    })
}
```

**wire/wire.go**

```go
//go:build wireinject
// +build wireinject

package wire

import (
    "wire-demo/internal/api"
    "wire-demo/internal/config"
    "wire-demo/internal/repository"
    "wire-demo/internal/router"
    "wire-demo/internal/service"
    "wire-demo/pkg/database"
    
    "github.com/gin-gonic/gin"
    "github.com/google/wire"
)

// App 应用结构体
type App struct {
    Engine *gin.Engine
    Config *config.Config
}

// InitializeApp 初始化应用
func InitializeApp() (*App, error) {
    wire.Build(
        // 配置和数据库
        config.LoadConfig,
        database.NewDatabase,
        
        // 各层依赖注入集合
        repository.RepositorySet,
        service.ServiceSet,
        api.APISet,
        
        // 路由
        router.SetupRouter,
        
        // 应用结构体
        wire.Struct(new(App), "*"),
    )
    return &App{}, nil
}
```

**cmd/server/main.go**

```go
package main

import (
    "context"
    "fmt"
    "log"
    "net/http"
    "os"
    "os/signal"
    "syscall"
    "time"
    "wire-demo/wire"
    
    "github.com/gin-gonic/gin"
)

func main() {
    app, err := wire.InitializeApp()
    if err != nil {
        log.Fatal("Failed to initialize app:", err)
    }
    
    // 设置 Gin 运行模式
    gin.SetMode(app.Config.Server.Mode)
    
    // 创建 HTTP 服务器
    addr := fmt.Sprintf(":%s", app.Config.Server.Port)
    srv := &http.Server{
        Addr:         addr,
        Handler:      app.Engine,
        ReadTimeout:  time.Duration(app.Config.Server.ReadTimeout) * time.Second,
        WriteTimeout: time.Duration(app.Config.Server.WriteTimeout) * time.Second,
    }
    
    // 在 goroutine 中启动服务器
    go func() {
        log.Printf("Server starting on %s in %s mode", addr, app.Config.Server.Mode)
        log.Printf("App: %s v%s", app.Config.App.Name, app.Config.App.Version)
        
        if err := srv.ListenAndServe(); err != nil && err != http.ErrServerClosed {
            log.Fatalf("Failed to start server: %v", err)
        }
    }()
    
    // 优雅关闭
    gracefulShutdown(srv)
}

func gracefulShutdown(srv *http.Server) {
    quit := make(chan os.Signal, 1)
    signal.Notify(quit, syscall.SIGINT, syscall.SIGTERM)
    <-quit
    
    log.Println("Shutting down server...")
    
    ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 30*time.Second)
    defer cancel()
    
    if err := srv.Shutdown(ctx); err != nil {
        log.Fatal("Server forced to shutdown:", err)
    }
    
    log.Println("Server exited")
}
```

### 使用说明


1. **安装依赖**：

```bash
go mod tidy
go install github.com/google/wire/cmd/wire@latest
go install github.com/golang/mock/mockgen@latest
```


2. **生成 Mock 文件**：

```bash
go generate ./...
```


3. **生成 Wire 代码**：

```bash
cd wire && wire
```


4. **运行测试**：

```bash
go test ./... -v
```


5. **运行服务**：

```bash
go run cmd/server/main.go
```

这个改造后的脚手架具有以下特点：

* 使用 `wire.NewSet` 组织各层依赖注入
* 完整的单元测试覆盖，使用 gomock 进行 mock
* 清晰的分层架构和依赖关系
* 支持优雅关闭和配置管理
* 遵循 Go 最佳实践

#### USER

wire wire: /Users/fatcat/workspace/go/src/github.com/featcat/wire-demo/module/internal/repository/wire.go:6:21: RepositorySet has multiple bindings for module/internal/repository.UserRepository current: <- wire.Bind (/Users/fatcat/workspace/go/src/github.com/featcat/wire-demo/module/internal/repository/wire.go:8:2) previous: <- provider "NewUserRepository" (/Users/fatcat/workspace/go/src/github.com/featcat/wire-demo/module/internal/repository/user.go:24:6) wire: /Users/fatcat/workspace/go/src/github.com/featcat/wire-demo/module/internal/service/wire.go:6:18: ServiceSet has multiple bindings for module/internal/service.UserService current: <- wire.Bind (/Users/fatcat/workspace/go/src/github.com/featcat/wire-demo/module/internal/service/wire.go:8:2) previous: <- provider "NewUserService" (/Users/fatcat/workspace/go/src/github.com/featcat/wire-demo/module/internal/service/user.go:24:6) wire: module/wire: generate failed wire: at least one generate failure

#### ASSISTANT

这个错误是因为 Wire 检测到了重复的绑定。当你的 `NewUserRepository` 函数返回的是接口类型时，Wire 会自动进行绑定，不需要再手动使用 `wire.Bind`。

让我修复这个问题：

### 修复方案

**方案一：移除 wire.Bind（推荐）**

**internal/repository/user_repository.go**

```go
package repository

import (
    "wire-demo/internal/model"
    "gorm.io/gorm"
)

//go:generate mockgen -source=user_repository.go -destination=../../mocks/mock_user_repository.go

type UserRepository interface {
    Create(user *model.User) error
    GetByID(id uint) (*model.User, error)
    GetByEmail(email string) (*model.User, error)
    Update(user *model.User) error
    Delete(id uint) error
    List(offset, limit int) ([]*model.User, int64, error)
    ExistsByEmail(email string) (bool, error)
}

type userRepository struct {
    db *gorm.DB
}

// NewUserRepository 直接返回接口类型，Wire 会自动处理绑定
func NewUserRepository(db *gorm.DB) UserRepository {
    return &userRepository{db: db}
}

// 实现方法保持不变...
func (r *userRepository) Create(user *model.User) error {
    return r.db.Create(user).Error
}

func (r *userRepository) GetByID(id uint) (*model.User, error) {
    var user model.User
    err := r.db.First(&user, id).Error
    if err != nil {
        return nil, err
    }
    return &user, nil
}

func (r *userRepository) GetByEmail(email string) (*model.User, error) {
    var user model.User
    err := r.db.Where("email = ?", email).First(&user).Error
    if err != nil {
        return nil, err
    }
    return &user, nil
}

func (r *userRepository) Update(user *model.User) error {
    return r.db.Save(user).Error
}

func (r *userRepository) Delete(id uint) error {
    return r.db.Delete(&model.User{}, id).Error
}

func (r *userRepository) List(offset, limit int) ([]*model.User, int64, error) {
    var users []*model.User
    var total int64
    
    if err := r.db.Model(&model.User{}).Count(&total).Error; err != nil {
        return nil, 0, err
    }
    
    err := r.db.Offset(offset).Limit(limit).Find(&users).Error
    return users, total, err
}

func (r *userRepository) ExistsByEmail(email string) (bool, error) {
    var count int64
    err := r.db.Model(&model.User{}).Where("email = ?", email).Count(&count).Error
    return count > 0, err
}
```

**internal/repository/wire.go**

```go
package repository

import "github.com/google/wire"

// RepositorySet 仓储层依赖注入集合
var RepositorySet = wire.NewSet(
    NewUserRepository,
    // 移除 wire.Bind，因为 NewUserRepository 已经返回接口类型
)
```

**internal/service/user_service.go**

```go
package service

import (
    "errors"
    "wire-demo/internal/model"
    "wire-demo/internal/repository"
    "gorm.io/gorm"
)

//go:generate mockgen -source=user_service.go -destination=../../mocks/mock_user_service.go

type UserService interface {
    CreateUser(req *model.CreateUserRequest) (*model.User, error)
    GetUser(id uint) (*model.User, error)
    UpdateUser(id uint, req *model.UpdateUserRequest) (*model.User, error)
    DeleteUser(id uint) error
    ListUsers(req *model.ListUsersRequest) (*model.ListUsersResponse, error)
}

type userService struct {
    userRepo repository.UserRepository
}

// NewUserService 直接返回接口类型，Wire 会自动处理绑定
func NewUserService(userRepo repository.UserRepository) UserService {
    return &userService{
        userRepo: userRepo,
    }
}

// 实现方法保持不变...
func (s *userService) CreateUser(req *model.CreateUserRequest) (*model.User, error) {
    exists, err := s.userRepo.ExistsByEmail(req.Email)
    if err != nil {
        return nil, err
    }
    if exists {
        return nil, errors.New("email already exists")
    }
    
    user := &model.User{
        Name:  req.Name,
        Email: req.Email,
    }
    
    if err := s.userRepo.Create(user); err != nil {
        return nil, err
    }
    
    return user, nil
}

func (s *userService) GetUser(id uint) (*model.User, error) {
    return s.userRepo.GetByID(id)
}

func (s *userService) UpdateUser(id uint, req *model.UpdateUserRequest) (*model.User, error) {
    user, err := s.userRepo.GetByID(id)
    if err != nil {
        if errors.Is(err, gorm.ErrRecordNotFound) {
            return nil, errors.New("user not found")
        }
        return nil, err
    }
    
    if req.Name != "" {
        user.Name = req.Name
    }
    if req.Email != "" {
        if existingUser, err := s.userRepo.GetByEmail(req.Email); err == nil && existingUser.ID != id {
            return nil, errors.New("email already exists")
        }
        user.Email = req.Email
    }
    
    if err := s.userRepo.Update(user); err != nil {
        return nil, err
    }
    
    return user, nil
}

func (s *userService) DeleteUser(id uint) error {
    if _, err := s.userRepo.GetByID(id); err != nil {
        if errors.Is(err, gorm.ErrRecordNotFound) {
            return errors.New("user not found")
        }
        return err
    }
    
    return s.userRepo.Delete(id)
}

func (s *userService) ListUsers(req *model.ListUsersRequest) (*model.ListUsersResponse, error) {
    offset := (req.Page - 1) * req.PageSize
    users, total, err := s.userRepo.List(offset, req.PageSize)
    if err != nil {
        return nil, err
    }
    
    return &model.ListUsersResponse{
        Users: users,
        Total: total,
        Page:  req.Page,
        Size:  req.PageSize,
    }, nil
}
```

**internal/service/wire.go**

```go
package service

import "github.com/google/wire"

// ServiceSet 服务层依赖注入集合
var ServiceSet = wire.NewSet(
    NewUserService,
    // 移除 wire.Bind，因为 NewUserService 已经返回接口类型
)
```

### 方案二：如果你想保留 wire.Bind（不推荐）

如果你坚持要使用 `wire.Bind`，那么需要让构造函数返回具体类型：

**internal/repository/user_repository.go**

```go
// 返回具体类型
func NewUserRepository(db *gorm.DB) *userRepository {
    return &userRepository{db: db}
}
```

**internal/repository/wire.go**

```go
package repository

import "github.com/google/wire"

var RepositorySet = wire.NewSet(
    NewUserRepository,
    wire.Bind(new(UserRepository), new(*userRepository)),
)
```

但是**推荐使用方案一**，因为：


1. 更简洁，不需要额外的绑定代码
2. Wire 自动处理接口绑定
3. 代码更清晰易懂
4. 符合 Go 的惯用法

### 修复后的完整 wire.go

**wire/wire.go**

```go
//go:build wireinject
// +build wireinject

package wire

import (
    "wire-demo/internal/api"
    "wire-demo/internal/config"
    "wire-demo/internal/repository"
    "wire-demo/internal/router"
    "wire-demo/internal/service"
    "wire-demo/pkg/database"
    
    "github.com/gin-gonic/gin"
    "github.com/google/wire"
)

// App 应用结构体
type App struct {
    Engine *gin.Engine
    Config *config.Config
}

// InitializeApp 初始化应用
func InitializeApp() (*App, error) {
    wire.Build(
        // 配置和数据库
        config.LoadConfig,
        database.NewDatabase,
        
        // 各层依赖注入集合
        repository.RepositorySet,
        service.ServiceSet,
        api.APISet,
        
        // 路由
        router.SetupRouter,
        
        // 应用结构体
        wire.Struct(new(App), "*"),
    )
    return &App{}, nil
}
```

现在运行 `wire` 命令应该不会再有错误了：

```bash
cd wire
wire
```

这样修复后，Wire 会正确生成依赖注入代码，不会有重复绑定的问题。

#### USER

**wire.Bind 的作用？以及wire.NewSet? 既然mocks中已经有generate.go了还需要在repository中的**go:generate 吗？

#### ASSISTANT

很好的问题！让我详细解释这些概念：

### 1. wire.Bind 的作用

`wire.Bind` 用于将具体类型绑定到接口类型，当构造函数返回具体类型但你需要接口类型时使用。

**示例对比：**

**不需要 wire.Bind 的情况（推荐）：**

```go
// 构造函数直接返回接口类型
func NewUserRepository(db *gorm.DB) UserRepository {
    return &userRepository{db: db}
}

// Wire 配置
var RepositorySet = wire.NewSet(
    NewUserRepository, // Wire 自动知道这返回 UserRepository 接口
)
```

**需要 wire.Bind 的情况：**

```go
// 构造函数返回具体类型
func NewUserRepository(db *gorm.DB) *userRepository {
    return &userRepository{db: db}
}

// Wire 配置 - 需要手动绑定
var RepositorySet = wire.NewSet(
    NewUserRepository,
    wire.Bind(new(UserRepository), new(*userRepository)), // 告诉 Wire：*userRepository 实现了 UserRepository
)
```

**wire.Bind 的语法：**

```go
wire.Bind(new(接口类型), new(具体类型))
```

### 2. wire.NewSet 的作用

`wire.NewSet` 用于创建一组相关的依赖注入提供者，可以包含：

* 构造函数
* 绑定关系
* 其他 NewSet

**示例：**

```go
// 基础 NewSet
var DatabaseSet = wire.NewSet(
    NewDatabase,
    NewRedis,
)

// 包含绑定的 NewSet
var RepositorySet = wire.NewSet(
    NewUserRepository,
    NewOrderRepository,
    wire.Bind(new(UserRepo), new(*userRepository)),
)

// 组合多个 NewSet
var AppSet = wire.NewSet(
    DatabaseSet,    // 引用其他 NewSet
    RepositorySet,
    ServiceSet,
)
```

**优势：**

* **模块化**：按层或功能组织依赖
* **复用性**：可以在不同的 wire 配置中复用
* **清晰性**：依赖关系更清楚

### 3. 关于 go:generate 的位置

你说得对！既然有了 `mocks/generate.go`，就不需要在每个文件中重复 `//go:generate`。

**推荐的组织方式：**

**mocks/generate.go**

```go
package mocks

// 统一管理所有 mock 生成
//go:generate mockgen -source=../internal/repository/user_repository.go -destination=mock_user_repository.go -package=mocks
//go:generate mockgen -source=../internal/service/user_service.go -destination=mock_user_service.go -package=mocks
//go:generate mockgen -source=../internal/api/handler/user_handler.go -destination=mock_user_handler.go -package=mocks
```

**移除其他文件中的 go:generate：**

**internal/repository/user_repository.go**

```go
package repository

import (
    "wire-demo/internal/model"
    "gorm.io/gorm"
)

// 移除这行：//go:generate mockgen -source=user_repository.go -destination=../../mocks/mock_user_repository.go

type UserRepository interface {
    Create(user *model.User) error
    GetByID(id uint) (*model.User, error)
    // ... 其他方法
}
```

**internal/service/user_service.go**

```go
package service

import (
    "wire-demo/internal/model"
    "wire-demo/internal/repository"
)

// 移除这行：//go:generate mockgen -source=user_service.go -destination=../../mocks/mock_user_service.go

type UserService interface {
    CreateUser(req *model.CreateUserRequest) (*model.User, error)
    // ... 其他方法
}
```

### 4. 完整的最佳实践示例

**internal/repository/wire.go**

```go
package repository

import "github.com/google/wire"

// RepositorySet 仓储层依赖注入集合
var RepositorySet = wire.NewSet(
    NewUserRepository,
    NewOrderRepository, // 假设还有其他 repository
    // 不需要 wire.Bind，因为构造函数返回接口类型
)
```

**internal/service/wire.go**

```go
package service

import "github.com/google/wire"

// ServiceSet 服务层依赖注入集合
var ServiceSet = wire.NewSet(
    NewUserService,
    NewOrderService, // 假设还有其他 service
)
```

**internal/api/wire.go**

```go
package api

import (
    "wire-demo/internal/api/handler"
    "github.com/google/wire"
)

// APISet API层依赖注入集合
var APISet = wire.NewSet(
    handler.NewUserHandler,
    handler.NewOrderHandler, // 假设还有其他 handler
)
```

**wire/wire.go**

```go
//go:build wireinject
// +build wireinject

package wire

import (
    "wire-demo/internal/api"
    "wire-demo/internal/config"
    "wire-demo/internal/repository"
    "wire-demo/internal/router"
    "wire-demo/internal/service"
    "wire-demo/pkg/database"
    
    "github.com/gin-gonic/gin"
    "github.com/google/wire"
)

type App struct {
    Engine *gin.Engine
    Config *config.Config
}

func InitializeApp() (*App, error) {
    wire.Build(
        // 基础设施
        config.LoadConfig,
        database.NewDatabase,
        
        // 业务层（使用 NewSet 组织）
        repository.RepositorySet,
        service.ServiceSet,
        api.APISet,
        
        // 路由和应用
        router.SetupRouter,
        wire.Struct(new(App), "*"),
    )
    return &App{}, nil
}
```

**mocks/generate.go**

```go
package mocks

//go:generate mockgen -source=../internal/repository/user_repository.go -destination=mock_user_repository.go -package=mocks
//go:generate mockgen -source=../internal/service/user_service.go -destination=mock_user_service.go -package=mocks
```

### 5. 生成命令

```bash
# 生成所有 mocks
go generate ./mocks/...

# 生成 wire 代码
cd wire && wire
```

### 总结

* **wire.Bind**：用于具体类型到接口的绑定，当构造函数返回接口类型时不需要
* **wire.NewSet**：用于组织相关的依赖提供者，提高模块化和复用性
* **go:generate**：建议集中在 `mocks/generate.go` 中管理，避免分散在各个文件中

这样的组织方式更清晰、更易维护。

#### USER

wire还有哪些常用的方法？

#### ASSISTANT

Wire 提供了多种方法来处理不同的依赖注入场景。让我详细介绍常用的方法：

### 1. 基础方法

#### wire.Build

用于定义依赖注入的构建规则

```go
func InitializeApp() (*App, error) {
    wire.Build(
        NewDatabase,
        NewUserRepository,
        NewUserService,
        NewApp,
    )
    return &App{}, nil
}
```

#### wire.NewSet

创建依赖提供者集合

```go
var DatabaseSet = wire.NewSet(
    NewDatabase,
    NewRedis,
    NewCache,
)
```

### 2. 绑定相关方法

#### wire.Bind

将具体类型绑定到接口

```go
var RepositorySet = wire.NewSet(
    NewUserRepository,
    wire.Bind(new(UserRepository), new(*userRepository)),
)
```

#### wire.BindMap

绑定到 map 类型

```go
// 定义 map 类型
type HandlerMap map[string]Handler

var HandlerSet = wire.NewSet(
    NewUserHandler,
    NewOrderHandler,
    wire.BindMap(new(HandlerMap), "user", new(*UserHandler)),
    wire.BindMap(new(HandlerMap), "order", new(*OrderHandler)),
)
```

### 3. 结构体相关方法

#### wire.Struct

创建结构体，自动注入字段

```go
type App struct {
    UserService  UserService
    OrderService OrderService
    Config       *Config
}

func InitializeApp() (*App, error) {
    wire.Build(
        NewConfig,
        NewUserService,
        NewOrderService,
        wire.Struct(new(App), "*"), // "*" 表示注入所有导出字段
    )
    return &App{}, nil
}

// 也可以指定特定字段
wire.Struct(new(App), "UserService", "Config")
```

#### wire.FieldsOf

从结构体中提取字段作为依赖

```go
type Config struct {
    Database DatabaseConfig
    Redis    RedisConfig
    Server   ServerConfig
}

var ConfigSet = wire.NewSet(
    LoadConfig,
    wire.FieldsOf(new(*Config), "Database", "Redis", "Server"),
)
```

### 4. 值提供方法

#### wire.Value

提供静态值

```go
var AppSet = wire.NewSet(
    wire.Value("localhost:8080"), // 提供字符串值
    wire.Value(30),               // 提供整数值
    NewServer,
)

func NewServer(addr string, timeout int) *Server {
    return &Server{Addr: addr, Timeout: timeout}
}
```

#### wire.InterfaceValue

提供接口值

```go
type Logger interface {
    Log(msg string)
}

type ConsoleLogger struct{}
func (c ConsoleLogger) Log(msg string) { fmt.Println(msg) }

var LoggerSet = wire.NewSet(
    wire.InterfaceValue(new(Logger), ConsoleLogger{}),
)
```

### 5. 高级用法示例

#### 条件注入

```go
//go:build wireinject
// +build wireinject

package wire

import (
    "os"
    "github.com/google/wire"
)

// 根据环境变量选择不同的实现
func InitializeApp() (*App, error) {
    if os.Getenv("ENV") == "production" {
        wire.Build(
            ProductionSet,
            wire.Struct(new(App), "*"),
        )
    } else {
        wire.Build(
            DevelopmentSet,
            wire.Struct(new(App), "*"),
        )
    }
    return &App{}, nil
}

var ProductionSet = wire.NewSet(
    NewProductionDatabase,
    NewProductionLogger,
    // ... 其他生产环境依赖
)

var DevelopmentSet = wire.NewSet(
    NewDevelopmentDatabase,
    NewDevelopmentLogger,
    // ... 其他开发环境依赖
)
```

#### 复杂的依赖关系

```go
type DatabaseConfig struct {
    Host     string
    Port     int
    Username string
    Password string
}

type Config struct {
    Database DatabaseConfig
    Redis    RedisConfig
    App      AppConfig
}

// 从配置中提取数据库配置
func ProvideDatabaseConfig(cfg *Config) DatabaseConfig {
    return cfg.Database
}

var AppSet = wire.NewSet(
    LoadConfig,
    ProvideDatabaseConfig,
    NewDatabase,
    NewUserRepository,
    NewUserService,
    wire.Struct(new(App), "*"),
)
```

#### 错误处理

```go
func NewDatabase(cfg DatabaseConfig) (*Database, error) {
    db, err := sql.Open("mysql", cfg.DSN())
    if err != nil {
        return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to open database: %w", err)
    }
    return &Database{db: db}, nil
}

func NewUserRepository(db *Database) (*UserRepository, error) {
    if db == nil {
        return nil, errors.New("database is required")
    }
    return &UserRepository{db: db}, nil
}
```

#### 多个相同类型的依赖

```go
// 使用不同的类型来区分
type PrimaryDB struct{ *sql.DB }
type SecondaryDB struct{ *sql.DB }

func NewPrimaryDB(cfg *Config) (*PrimaryDB, error) {
    db, err := sql.Open("mysql", cfg.PrimaryDSN)
    return &PrimaryDB{db}, err
}

func NewSecondaryDB(cfg *Config) (*SecondaryDB, error) {
    db, err := sql.Open("mysql", cfg.SecondaryDSN)
    return &SecondaryDB{db}, err
}

func NewUserRepository(primary *PrimaryDB, secondary *SecondaryDB) *UserRepository {
    return &UserRepository{
        primary:   primary.DB,
        secondary: secondary.DB,
    }
}
```

### 6. 实际项目中的完整示例

```go
//go:build wireinject
// +build wireinject

package wire

import (
    "myapp/internal/api"
    "myapp/internal/config"
    "myapp/internal/repository"
    "myapp/internal/service"
    "myapp/pkg/database"
    "myapp/pkg/logger"
    "myapp/pkg/cache"
    
    "github.com/google/wire"
)

// 基础设施层
var InfrastructureSet = wire.NewSet(
    config.Load,
    database.NewMySQL,
    database.NewRedis,
    logger.NewZapLogger,
    cache.NewRedisCache,
    wire.Bind(new(logger.Logger), new(*logger.ZapLogger)),
    wire.Bind(new(cache.Cache), new(*cache.RedisCache)),
)

// 仓储层
var RepositorySet = wire.NewSet(
    repository.NewUserRepository,
    repository.NewOrderRepository,
    repository.NewProductRepository,
)

// 服务层
var ServiceSet = wire.NewSet(
    service.NewUserService,
    service.NewOrderService,
    service.NewProductService,
    service.NewEmailService,
)

// API 层
var APISet = wire.NewSet(
    api.NewUserHandler,
    api.NewOrderHandler,
    api.NewProductHandler,
)

// 应用结构
type App struct {
    UserHandler    *api.UserHandler
    OrderHandler   *api.OrderHandler
    ProductHandler *api.ProductHandler
    Config         *config.Config
    Logger         logger.Logger
}

// 主应用初始化
func InitializeApp() (*App, error) {
    wire.Build(
        InfrastructureSet,
        RepositorySet,
        ServiceSet,
        APISet,
        wire.Struct(new(App), "*"),
    )
    return &App{}, nil
}

// 测试用的初始化（使用 mock）
func InitializeTestApp() (*App, error) {
    wire.Build(
        config.LoadTest,
        database.NewMockDB,
        logger.NewTestLogger,
        RepositorySet,
        ServiceSet,
        APISet,
        wire.Struct(new(App), "*"),
    )
    return &App{}, nil
}
```

### 7. 常见模式和最佳实践

#### Provider 函数模式

```go
// 提供者函数可以有多个参数和返回值
func NewUserService(
    repo UserRepository,
    logger Logger,
    config *Config,
) (*UserService, error) {
    if repo == nil {
        return nil, errors.New("repository is required")
    }
    
    return &UserService{
        repo:   repo,
        logger: logger,
        config: config,
    }, nil
}
```

#### 清理资源模式

```go
type App struct {
    db     *sql.DB
    server *http.Server
}

func (a *App) Close() error {
    if a.server != nil {
        a.server.Close()
    }
    if a.db != nil {
        a.db.Close()
    }
    return nil
}
```

这些是 Wire 的主要方法和使用模式。Wire 的设计哲学是在编译时解决依赖注入，这样可以避免运行时的反射开销，同时提供编译时的类型安全检查。

#### USER

我发现上面的\*_test.go中的代码并没有使用mocks

#### ASSISTANT

你说得对！我在上面的测试代码中确实有混淆。让我重新整理，明确区分哪些测试需要使用 mocks，哪些不需要：

### 测试分类

#### 1. Repository 层测试 - 不使用 mocks（集成测试）

Repository 层直接测试与数据库的交互，使用内存数据库

#### 2. Service 层测试 - 使用 mocks（单元测试）

Service 层 mock repository 接口

#### 3. Handler 层测试 - 使用 mocks（单元测试）

Handler 层 mock service 接口

### 修正后的测试代码

#### Repository 测试（保持不变，使用真实数据库）

**internal/repository/user_repository_test.go**

```go
package repository

import (
    "testing"
    "wire-demo/internal/model"
    
    "github.com/stretchr/testify/assert"
    "github.com/stretchr/testify/suite"
    "gorm.io/driver/sqlite"
    "gorm.io/gorm"
)

type UserRepositoryTestSuite struct {
    suite.Suite
    db   *gorm.DB
    repo UserRepository
}

func (suite *UserRepositoryTestSuite) SetupSuite() {
    db, err := gorm.Open(sqlite.Open(":memory:"), &gorm.Config{})
    suite.Require().NoError(err)
    
    err = db.AutoMigrate(&model.User{})
    suite.Require().NoError(err)
    
    suite.db = db
    suite.repo = NewUserRepository(db)
}

func (suite *UserRepositoryTestSuite) SetupTest() {
    suite.db.Exec("DELETE FROM users")
}

func (suite *UserRepositoryTestSuite) TestCreate() {
    user := &model.User{
        Name:  "Test User",
        Email: "test@example.com",
    }
    
    err := suite.repo.Create(user)
    assert.NoError(suite.T(), err)
    assert.NotZero(suite.T(), user.ID)
}

func (suite *UserRepositoryTestSuite) TestGetByID() {
    user := &model.User{
        Name:  "Test User",
        Email: "test@example.com",
    }
    err := suite.repo.Create(user)
    suite.Require().NoError(err)
    
    foundUser, err := suite.repo.GetByID(user.ID)
    assert.NoError(suite.T(), err)
    assert.Equal(suite.T(), user.Name, foundUser.Name)
    assert.Equal(suite.T(), user.Email, foundUser.Email)
}

func TestUserRepositoryTestSuite(t *testing.T) {
    suite.Run(t, new(UserRepositoryTestSuite))
}
```

#### Service 测试（使用 Repository Mock）

**internal/service/user_service_test.go**

```go
package service

import (
    "errors"
    "testing"
    "wire-demo/internal/model"
    "wire-demo/mocks"
    
    "github.com/golang/mock/gomock"
    "github.com/stretchr/testify/assert"
    "gorm.io/gorm"
)

func TestUserService_CreateUser(t *testing.T) {
    ctrl := gomock.NewController(t)
    defer ctrl.Finish()
    
    mockRepo := mocks.NewMockUserRepository(ctrl)
    service := NewUserService(mockRepo)
    
    t.Run("success", func(t *testing.T) {
        req := &model.CreateUserRequest{
            Name:  "Test User",
            Email: "test@example.com",
        }
        
        // Mock: 检查邮箱不存在
        mockRepo.EXPECT().
            ExistsByEmail(req.Email).
            Return(false, nil)
        
        // Mock: 创建用户成功
        mockRepo.EXPECT().
            Create(gomock.Any()).
            DoAndReturn(func(user *model.User) error {
                user.ID = 1 // 模拟数据库分配 ID
                return nil
            })
        
        user, err := service.CreateUser(req)
        assert.NoError(t, err)
        assert.Equal(t, req.Name, user.Name)
        assert.Equal(t, req.Email, user.Email)
        assert.Equal(t, uint(1), user.ID)
    })
    
    t.Run("email already exists", func(t *testing.T) {
        req := &model.CreateUserRequest{
            Name:  "Test User",
            Email: "existing@example.com",
        }
        
        // Mock: 邮箱已存在
        mockRepo.EXPECT().
            ExistsByEmail(req.Email).
            Return(true, nil)
        
        user, err := service.CreateUser(req)
        assert.Error(t, err)
        assert.Nil(t, user)
        assert.Contains(t, err.Error(), "email already exists")
    })
    
    t.Run("repository error on check email", func(t *testing.T) {
        req := &model.CreateUserRequest{
            Name:  "Test User",
            Email: "test@example.com",
        }
        
        // Mock: 检查邮箱时发生错误
        mockRepo.EXPECT().
            ExistsByEmail(req.Email).
            Return(false, errors.New("database connection error"))
        
        user, err := service.CreateUser(req)
        assert.Error(t, err)
        assert.Nil(t, user)
        assert.Contains(t, err.Error(), "database connection error")
    })
}

func TestUserService_GetUser(t *testing.T) {
    ctrl := gomock.NewController(t)
    defer ctrl.Finish()
    
    mockRepo := mocks.NewMockUserRepository(ctrl)
    service := NewUserService(mockRepo)
    
    t.Run("success", func(t *testing.T) {
        expectedUser := &model.User{
            ID:    1,
            Name:  "Test User",
            Email: "test@example.com",
        }
        
        mockRepo.EXPECT().
            GetByID(uint(1)).
            Return(expectedUser, nil)
        
        user, err := service.GetUser(1)
        assert.NoError(t, err)
        assert.Equal(t, expectedUser, user)
    })
    
    t.Run("user not found", func(t *testing.T) {
        mockRepo.EXPECT().
            GetByID(uint(999)).
            Return(nil, gorm.ErrRecordNotFound)
        
        user, err := service.GetUser(999)
        assert.Error(t, err)
        assert.Nil(t, user)
        assert.Equal(t, gorm.ErrRecordNotFound, err)
    })
}

func TestUserService_UpdateUser(t *testing.T) {
    ctrl := gomock.NewController(t)
    defer ctrl.Finish()
    
    mockRepo := mocks.NewMockUserRepository(ctrl)
    service := NewUserService(mockRepo)
    
    t.Run("success - update name only", func(t *testing.T) {
        existingUser := &model.User{
            ID:    1,
            Name:  "Old Name",
            Email: "test@example.com",
        }
        
        req := &model.UpdateUserRequest{
            Name: "New Name",
        }
        
        mockRepo.EXPECT().
            GetByID(uint(1)).
            Return(existingUser, nil)
        
        mockRepo.EXPECT().
            Update(gomock.Any()).
            DoAndReturn(func(user *model.User) error {
                assert.Equal(t, "New Name", user.Name)
                assert.Equal(t, "test@example.com", user.Email) // 邮箱不变
                return nil
            })
        
        user, err := service.UpdateUser(1, req)
        assert.NoError(t, err)
        assert.Equal(t, "New Name", user.Name)
        assert.Equal(t, "test@example.com", user.Email)
    })
    
    t.Run("success - update email", func(t *testing.T) {
        existingUser := &model.User{
            ID:    1,
            Name:  "Test User",
            Email: "old@example.com",
        }
        
        req := &model.UpdateUserRequest{
            Email: "new@example.com",
        }
        
        mockRepo.EXPECT().
            GetByID(uint(1)).
            Return(existingUser, nil)
        
        // Mock: 检查新邮箱不存在
        mockRepo.EXPECT().
            GetByEmail("new@example.com").
            Return(nil, gorm.ErrRecordNotFound)
        
        mockRepo.EXPECT().
            Update(gomock.Any()).
            Return(nil)
        
        user, err := service.UpdateUser(1, req)
        assert.NoError(t, err)
        assert.Equal(t, "new@example.com", user.Email)
    })
    
    t.Run("email already exists for another user", func(t *testing.T) {
        existingUser := &model.User{
            ID:    1,
            Name:  "Test User",
            Email: "old@example.com",
        }
        
        anotherUser := &model.User{
            ID:    2,
            Name:  "Another User",
            Email: "existing@example.com",
        }
        
        req := &model.UpdateUserRequest{
            Email: "existing@example.com",
        }
        
        mockRepo.EXPECT().
            GetByID(uint(1)).
            Return(existingUser, nil)
        
        // Mock: 邮箱已被其他用户使用
        mockRepo.EXPECT().
            GetByEmail("existing@example.com").
            Return(anotherUser, nil)
        
        user, err := service.UpdateUser(1, req)
        assert.Error(t, err)
        assert.Nil(t, user)
        assert.Contains(t, err.Error(), "email already exists")
    })
    
    t.Run("user not found", func(t *testing.T) {
        req := &model.UpdateUserRequest{
            Name: "New Name",
        }
        
        mockRepo.EXPECT().
            GetByID(uint(999)).
            Return(nil, gorm.ErrRecordNotFound)
        
        user, err := service.UpdateUser(999, req)
        assert.Error(t, err)
        assert.Nil(t, user)
        assert.Contains(t, err.Error(), "user not found")
    })
}
```
