#### 1. 复杂的状态机和工作流测试

**问题：难以测试的状态机**

```go
// 难以测试的订单状态机
type OrderStateMachine struct {
    order *Order
    db    *sql.DB
    email *EmailService
    sms   *SMSService
    audit *AuditService
}

func (sm *OrderStateMachine) ProcessPayment(paymentID string) error {
    // 复杂的状态转换逻辑
    switch sm.order.Status {
    case "pending":
        // 调用支付服务
        payment, err := http.Get("https://payment-api.com/payments/" + paymentID)
        if err != nil {
            return err
        }
        
        if payment.StatusCode == 200 {
            sm.order.Status = "paid"
            sm.order.PaidAt = time.Now()
            
            // 更新数据库
            _, err = sm.db.Exec("UPDATE orders SET status = ?, paid_at = ? WHERE id = ?", 
                sm.order.Status, sm.order.PaidAt, sm.order.ID)
            if err != nil {
                return err
            }
            
            // 发送邮件
            sm.email.SendPaymentConfirmation(sm.order.CustomerEmail, sm.order.ID)
            
            // 发送短信
            if sm.order.CustomerPhone != "" {
                sm.sms.SendPaymentNotification(sm.order.CustomerPhone, sm.order.ID)
            }
            
            // 记录审计日志
            sm.audit.LogOrderStatusChange(sm.order.ID, "pending", "paid", "payment_processed")
            
            // 如果是预订商品，需要检查库存
            if sm.order.Type == "preorder" {
                inventory, err := http.Get("https://inventory-api.com/check/" + sm.order.ProductID)
                if err == nil && inventory.StatusCode == 200 {
                    sm.order.Status = "confirmed"
                    sm.db.Exec("UPDATE orders SET status = ? WHERE id = ?", sm.order.Status, sm.order.ID)
                }
            }
        } else {
            sm.order.Status = "payment_failed"
            sm.db.Exec("UPDATE orders SET status = ? WHERE id = ?", sm.order.Status, sm.order.ID)
            sm.email.SendPaymentFailureNotification(sm.order.CustomerEmail, sm.order.ID)
        }
    case "paid":
        return errors.New("order already paid")
    default:
        return errors.New("invalid order status for payment")
    }
    
    return nil
}
```

**解决方案：状态机模式 + 依赖注入**

```go
// 定义状态和事件
type OrderStatus string
type OrderEvent string

const (
    StatusPending       OrderStatus = "pending"
    StatusPaid         OrderStatus = "paid"
    StatusConfirmed    OrderStatus = "confirmed"
    StatusPaymentFailed OrderStatus = "payment_failed"
    StatusCancelled    OrderStatus = "cancelled"
)

const (
    EventPaymentReceived OrderEvent = "payment_received"
    EventPaymentFailed   OrderEvent = "payment_failed"
    EventInventoryConfirmed OrderEvent = "inventory_confirmed"
    EventCancelled       OrderEvent = "cancelled"
)

// 定义依赖接口
type PaymentService interface {
    GetPayment(paymentID string) (*Payment, error)
}

type InventoryService interface {
    CheckInventory(productID string) (*InventoryStatus, error)
}

type NotificationService interface {
    SendPaymentConfirmation(email string, orderID int) error
    SendPaymentFailure(email string, orderID int) error
    SendSMS(phone string, message string) error
}

type OrderRepository interface {
    UpdateStatus(orderID int, status OrderStatus) error
    UpdatePaidAt(orderID int, paidAt time.Time) error
}

type AuditLogger interface {
    LogStatusChange(orderID int, from, to OrderStatus, reason string) error
}

// 状态转换定义
type StateTransition struct {
    From   OrderStatus
    Event  OrderEvent
    To     OrderStatus
    Action func(ctx *TransitionContext) error
}

type TransitionContext struct {
    Order       *Order
    Event       OrderEvent
    EventData   interface{}
    Services    *Services
}

type Services struct {
    Payment      PaymentService
    Inventory    InventoryService
    Notification NotificationService
    Repository   OrderRepository
    Audit        AuditLogger
    Clock        Clock
}

// 可测试的状态机
type OrderStateMachine struct {
    transitions map[string]StateTransition
    services    *Services
}

func NewOrderStateMachine(services *Services) *OrderStateMachine {
    sm := &OrderStateMachine{
        services:    services,
        transitions: make(map[string]StateTransition),
    }
    
    sm.registerTransitions()
    return sm
}

func (sm *OrderStateMachine) registerTransitions() {
    sm.addTransition(StateTransition{
        From:   StatusPending,
        Event:  EventPaymentReceived,
        To:     StatusPaid,
        Action: sm.handlePaymentReceived,
    })
    
    sm.addTransition(StateTransition{
        From:   StatusPending,
        Event:  EventPaymentFailed,
        To:     StatusPaymentFailed,
        Action: sm.handlePaymentFailed,
    })
    
    sm.addTransition(StateTransition{
        From:   StatusPaid,
        Event:  EventInventoryConfirmed,
        To:     StatusConfirmed,
        Action: sm.handleInventoryConfirmed,
    })
}

func (sm *OrderStateMachine) addTransition(transition StateTransition) {
    key := string(transition.From) + ":" + string(transition.Event)
    sm.transitions[key] = transition
}

func (sm *OrderStateMachine) ProcessEvent(order *Order, event OrderEvent, eventData interface{}) error {
    key := string(order.Status) + ":" + string(event)
    transition, exists := sm.transitions[key]
    if !exists {
        return fmt.Errorf("invalid transition from %s with event %s", order.Status, event)
    }
    
    ctx := &TransitionContext{
        Order:     order,
        Event:     event,
        EventData: eventData,
        Services:  sm.services,
    }
    
    // 执行转换动作
    if err := transition.Action(ctx); err != nil {
        return err
    }
    
    // 更新状态
    oldStatus := order.Status
    order.Status = transition.To
    
    // 保存到数据库
    if err := sm.services.Repository.UpdateStatus(order.ID, order.Status); err != nil {
        return err
    }
    
    // 记录审计日志
    return sm.services.Audit.LogStatusChange(order.ID, oldStatus, order.Status, string(event))
}

func (sm *OrderStateMachine) handlePaymentReceived(ctx *TransitionContext) error {
    paymentData := ctx.EventData.(*PaymentData)
    
    // 验证支付
    payment, err := sm.services.Payment.GetPayment(paymentData.PaymentID)
    if err != nil {
        return err
    }
    
    if payment.Status != "completed" {
        return errors.New("payment not completed")
    }
    
    // 更新支付时间
    paidAt := sm.services.Clock.Now()
    ctx.Order.PaidAt = &paidAt
    
    if err := sm.services.Repository.UpdatePaidAt(ctx.Order.ID, paidAt); err != nil {
        return err
    }
    
    // 发送通知
    if err := sm.services.Notification.SendPaymentConfirmation(ctx.Order.CustomerEmail, ctx.Order.ID); err != nil {
        // 记录错误但不失败
        log.Printf("Failed to send payment confirmation: %v", err)
    }
    
    // 如果是预订商品，检查库存
    if ctx.Order.Type == "preorder" {
        go sm.checkInventoryAsync(ctx.Order)
    }
    
    return nil
}

func (sm *OrderStateMachine) handlePaymentFailed(ctx *TransitionContext) error {
    // 发送失败通知
    return sm.services.Notification.SendPaymentFailure(ctx.Order.CustomerEmail, ctx.Order.ID)
}

func (sm *OrderStateMachine) handleInventoryConfirmed(ctx *TransitionContext) error {
    // 库存确认后的处理逻辑
    return nil
}

// 测试代码
func TestOrderStateMachine_ProcessPayment(t *testing.T) {
    ctrl := gomock.NewController(t)
    defer ctrl.Finish()
    
    // 创建所有 mock 服务
    mockPayment := mocks.NewMockPaymentService(ctrl)
    mockInventory := mocks.NewMockInventoryService(ctrl)
    mockNotification := mocks.NewMockNotificationService(ctrl)
    mockRepository := mocks.NewMockOrderRepository(ctrl)
    mockAudit := mocks.NewMockAuditLogger(ctrl)
    mockClock := &MockClock{current: time.Date(2023, 1, 1, 12, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC)}
    
    services := &Services{
        Payment:      mockPayment,
        Inventory:    mockInventory,
        Notification: mockNotification,
        Repository:   mockRepository,
        Audit:        mockAudit,
        Clock:        mockClock,
    }
    
    sm := NewOrderStateMachine(services)
    
    t.Run("successful payment processing", func(t *testing.T) {
        order := &Order{
            ID:            1,
            Status:        StatusPending,
            CustomerEmail: "customer@example.com",
            Type:          "normal",
        }
        
        paymentData := &PaymentData{PaymentID: "pay_123"}
        
        // 设置 mock 期望
        mockPayment.EXPECT().
            GetPayment("pay_123").
            Return(&Payment{Status: "completed"}, nil)
        
        mockRepository.EXPECT().
            UpdatePaidAt(1, mockClock.current).
            Return(nil)
        
        mockRepository.EXPECT().
            UpdateStatus(1, StatusPaid).
            Return(nil)
        
        mockNotification.EXPECT().
            SendPaymentConfirmation("customer@example.com", 1).
            Return(nil)
        
        mockAudit.EXPECT().
            LogStatusChange(1, StatusPending, StatusPaid, "payment_received").
            Return(nil)
        
        err := sm.ProcessEvent(order, EventPaymentReceived, paymentData)
        
        assert.NoError(t, err)
        assert.Equal(t, StatusPaid, order.Status)
        assert.Equal(t, mockClock.current, *order.PaidAt)
    })
    
    t.Run("payment failed", func(t *testing.T) {
        order := &Order{
            ID:            1,
            Status:        StatusPending,
            CustomerEmail: "customer@example.com",
        }
        
        mockNotification.EXPECT().
            SendPaymentFailure("customer@example.com", 1).
            Return(nil)
        
        mockRepository.EXPECT().
            UpdateStatus(1, StatusPaymentFailed).
            Return(nil)
        
        mockAudit.EXPECT().
            LogStatusChange(1, StatusPending, StatusPaymentFailed, "payment_failed").
            Return(nil)
        
        err := sm.ProcessEvent(order, EventPaymentFailed, nil)
        
        assert.NoError(t, err)
        assert.Equal(t, StatusPaymentFailed, order.Status)
    })
    
    t.Run("invalid state transition", func(t *testing.T) {
        order := &Order{
            ID:     1,
            Status: StatusPaid, // 已支付状态
        }
        
        err := sm.ProcessEvent(order, EventPaymentReceived, nil)
        
        assert.Error(t, err)
        assert.Contains(t, err.Error(), "invalid transition")
    })
}
```

#### 2. 复杂的权限和认证系统测试

**问题：难以测试的权限系统**

```go
// 难以测试的权限检查
type AuthService struct {
    jwtSecret string
    db        *sql.DB
    redis     *redis.Client
}

func (a *AuthService) CheckPermission(token string, resource string, action string) bool {
    // 解析 JWT token
    claims, err := jwt.Parse(token, func(token *jwt.Token) (interface{}, error) {
        return []byte(a.jwtSecret), nil
    })
    if err != nil {
        return false
    }
    
    userID := claims.Claims.(jwt.MapClaims)["user_id"].(float64)
    
    // 检查用户是否存在
    var exists bool
    a.db.QueryRow("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM users WHERE id = ?)", userID).Scan(&exists)
    if !exists {
        return false
    }
    
    // 检查缓存中的权限
    cacheKey := fmt.Sprintf("permissions:%d", int(userID))
    permissions, err := a.redis.Get(context.Background(), cacheKey).Result()
    if err == nil {
        // 解析缓存的权限
        var perms []Permission
        json.Unmarshal([]byte(permissions), &perms)
        
        for _, perm := range perms {
            if perm.Resource == resource && perm.Action == action {
                return true
            }
        }
        return false
    }
    
    // 从数据库查询权限
    rows, err := a.db.Query(`
        SELECT p.resource, p.action 
        FROM permissions p
        JOIN user_roles ur ON ur.role_id = p.role_id
        WHERE ur.user_id = ?`, userID)
    if err != nil {
        return false
    }
    defer rows.Close()
    
    var userPermissions []Permission
    for rows.Next() {
        var perm Permission
        rows.Scan(&perm.Resource, &perm.Action)
        userPermissions = append(userPermissions, perm)
        
        if perm.Resource == resource && perm.Action == action {
            // 缓存权限
            permData, _ := json.Marshal(userPermissions)
            a.redis.Set(context.Background(), cacheKey, permData, time.Hour)
            return true
        }
    }
    
    // 缓存空权限
    a.redis.Set(context.Background(), cacheKey, "[]", time.Hour)
    return false
}
```

**解决方案：分离关注点 + 依赖注入**

```go
// 定义认证和授权接口
type TokenValidator interface {
    ValidateToken(token string) (*Claims, error)
}

type UserRepository interface {
    UserExists(userID int) (bool, error)
}

type PermissionRepository interface {
    GetUserPermissions(userID int) ([]Permission, error)
}

type PermissionCache interface {
    GetPermissions(userID int) ([]Permission, error)
    SetPermissions(userID int, permissions []Permission, ttl time.Duration) error
}

type Claims struct {
    UserID int    `json:"user_id"`
    Email  string `json:"email"`
    Exp    int64  `json:"exp"`
}

type Permission struct {
    Resource string `json:"resource"`
    Action   string `json:"action"`
}

// JWT Token 验证器
type JWTValidator struct {
    secret []byte
}

func NewJWTValidator(secret string) *JWTValidator {
    return &JWTValidator{secret: []byte(secret)}
}

func (v *JWTValidator) ValidateToken(tokenString string) (*Claims, error) {
    token, err := jwt.ParseWithClaims(tokenString, &Claims{}, func(token *jwt.Token) (interface{}, error) {
        if _, ok := token.Method.(*jwt.SigningMethodHMAC); !ok {
            return nil, fmt.Errorf("unexpected signing method: %v", token.Header["alg"])
        }
        return v.secret, nil
    })
    
    if err != nil {
        return nil, err
    }
    
    if claims, ok := token.Claims.(*Claims); ok && token.Valid {
        // 检查过期时间
        if time.Now().Unix() > claims.Exp {
            return nil, errors.New("token expired")
        }
        return claims, nil
    }
    
    return nil, errors.New("invalid token")
}

// Redis 权限缓存
type RedisPermissionCache struct {
    client redis.Cmdable
}

func NewRedisPermissionCache(client redis.Cmdable) *RedisPermissionCache {
    return &RedisPermissionCache{client: client}
}

func (c *RedisPermissionCache) GetPermissions(userID int) ([]Permission, error) {
    key := fmt.Sprintf("permissions:%d", userID)
    data, err := c.client.Get(context.Background(), key).Result()
    if err != nil {
        return nil, err
    }
    
    var permissions []Permission
    err = json.Unmarshal([]byte(data), &permissions)
    return permissions, err
}

func (c *RedisPermissionCache) SetPermissions(userID int, permissions []Permission, ttl time.Duration) error {
    key := fmt.Sprintf("permissions:%d", userID)
    data, err := json.Marshal(permissions)
    if err != nil {
        return err
    }
    
    return c.client.Set(context.Background(), key, data, ttl).Err()
}

// 可测试的认证服务
type AuthService struct {
    tokenValidator TokenValidator
    userRepo       UserRepository
    permRepo       PermissionRepository
    permCache      PermissionCache
}

func NewAuthService(
    tokenValidator TokenValidator,
    userRepo UserRepository,
    permRepo PermissionRepository,
    permCache PermissionCache,
) *AuthService {
    return &AuthService{
        tokenValidator: tokenValidator,
        userRepo:       userRepo,
        permRepo:       permRepo,
        permCache:      permCache,
    }
}

func (a *AuthService) CheckPermission(token string, resource string, action string) (bool, error) {
    // 验证 token
    claims, err := a.tokenValidator.ValidateToken(token)
    if err != nil {
        return false, fmt.Errorf("invalid token: %w", err)
    }
    
    // 检查用户是否存在
    exists, err := a.userRepo.UserExists(claims.UserID)
    if err != nil {
        return false, fmt.Errorf("failed to check user existence: %w", err)
    }
    if !exists {
        return false, errors.New("user not found")
    }
    
    // 获取用户权限
    permissions, err := a.getUserPermissions(claims.UserID)
    if err != nil {
        return false, fmt.Errorf("failed to get user permissions: %w", err)
    }
    
    // 检查权限
    return a.hasPermission(permissions, resource, action), nil
}

func (a *AuthService) getUserPermissions(userID int) ([]Permission, error) {
    // 先尝试从缓存获取
    permissions, err := a.permCache.GetPermissions(userID)
    if err == nil {
        return permissions, nil
    }
    
    // 从数据库获取
    permissions, err = a.permRepo.GetUserPermissions(userID)
    if err != nil {
        return nil, err
    }
    
    // 写入缓存
    a.permCache.SetPermissions(userID, permissions, time.Hour)
    
    return permissions, nil
}

func (a *AuthService) hasPermission(permissions []Permission, resource, action string) bool {
    for _, perm := range permissions {
        if perm.Resource == resource && perm.Action == action {
            return true
        }
        // 支持通配符权限
        if perm.Resource == "*" || perm.Action == "*" {
            return true
        }
    }
    return false
}

// 测试代码
func TestAuthService_CheckPermission(t *testing.T) {
    ctrl := gomock.NewController(t)
    defer ctrl.Finish()
    
    mockTokenValidator := mocks.NewMockTokenValidator(ctrl)
    mockUserRepo := mocks.NewMockUserRepository(ctrl)
    mockPermRepo := mocks.NewMockPermissionRepository(ctrl)
    mockPermCache := mocks.NewMockPermissionCache(ctrl)
    
    authService := NewAuthService(mockTokenValidator, mockUserRepo, mockPermRepo, mockPermCache)
    
    t.Run("valid token and permission", func(t *testing.T) {
        token := "valid.jwt.token"
        claims := &Claims{UserID: 1, Email: "user@example.com", Exp: time.Now().Add(time.Hour).Unix()}
        permissions := []Permission{
            {Resource: "users", Action: "read"},
            {Resource: "users", Action: "write"},
        }
        
        mockTokenValidator.EXPECT().
            ValidateToken(token).
            Return(claims, nil)
        
        mockUserRepo.EXPECT().
            UserExists(1).
            Return(true, nil)
        
        mockPermCache.EXPECT().
            GetPermissions(1).
            Return(nil, errors.New("cache miss"))
        
        mockPermRepo.EXPECT().
            GetUserPermissions(1).
            Return(permissions, nil)
        
        mockPermCache.EXPECT().
            SetPermissions(1, permissions, time.Hour).
            Return(nil)
        
        hasPermission, err := authService.CheckPermission(token, "users", "read")
        
        assert.NoError(t, err)
        assert.True(t, hasPermission)
    })
    
    t.Run("invalid token", func(t *testing.T) {
        token := "invalid.token"
        
        mockTokenValidator.EXPECT().
            ValidateToken(token).
            Return(nil, errors.New("invalid token"))
        
        hasPermission, err := authService.CheckPermission(token, "users", "read")
        
        assert.Error(t, err)
        assert.False(t, hasPermission)
        assert.Contains(t, err.Error(), "invalid token")
    })
    
    t.Run("user not found", func(t *testing.T) {
        token := "valid.jwt.token"
        claims := &Claims{UserID: 999, Email: "user@example.com", Exp: time.Now().Add(time.Hour).Unix()}
        
        mockTokenValidator.EXPECT().
            ValidateToken(token).
            Return(claims, nil)
        
        mockUserRepo.EXPECT().
            UserExists(999).
            Return(false, nil)
        
        hasPermission, err := authService.CheckPermission(token, "users", "read")
        
        assert.Error(t, err)
        assert.False(t, hasPermission)
        assert.Contains(t, err.Error(), "user not found")
    })
    
    t.Run("permission denied", func(t *testing.T) {
        token := "valid.jwt.token"
        claims := &Claims{UserID: 1, Email: "user@example.com", Exp: time.Now().Add(time.Hour).Unix()}
        permissions := []Permission{
            {Resource: "users", Action: "read"},
        }
        
        mockTokenValidator.EXPECT().
            ValidateToken(token).
            Return(claims, nil)
        
        mockUserRepo.EXPECT().
            UserExists(1).
            Return(true, nil)
        
        mockPermCache.EXPECT().
            GetPermissions(1).
            Return(permissions, nil) // 从缓存获取
        
        hasPermission, err := authService.CheckPermission(token, "users", "delete")
        
        assert.NoError(t, err)
        assert.False(t, hasPermission)
    })
    
    t.Run("wildcard permission", func(t *testing.T) {
        token := "valid.jwt.token"
        claims := &Claims{UserID: 1, Email: "admin@example.com", Exp: time.Now().Add(time.Hour).Unix()}
        permissions := []Permission{
            {Resource: "*", Action: "*"}, // 管理员权限
        }
        
        mockTokenValidator.EXPECT().
            ValidateToken(token).
            Return(claims, nil)
        
        mockUserRepo.EXPECT().
            UserExists(1).
            Return(true, nil)
        
        mockPermCache.EXPECT().
            GetPermissions(1).
            Return(permissions, nil)
        
        hasPermission, err := authService.CheckPermission(token, "any_resource", "any_action")
        
        assert.NoError(t, err)
        assert.True(t, hasPermission)
    })
}
```

#### 3. 复杂的数据处理和ETL流程测试

**问题：难以测试的数据处理流程**

```go
// 难以测试的数据处理流程
type DataProcessor struct {
    inputDir  string
    outputDir string
    db        *sql.DB
    s3Client  *s3.S3
}

func (dp *DataProcessor) ProcessDailyData(date time.Time) error {
    // 从多个源读取数据
    files, err := filepath.Glob(dp.inputDir + "/data_" + date.Format("2006-01-02") + "_*.csv")
    if err != nil {
        return err
    }
    
    var allRecords []DataRecord
    
    for _, file := range files {
        // 读取 CSV 文件
        csvFile, err := os.Open(file)
        if err != nil {
            continue
        }
        defer csvFile.Close()
        
        reader := csv.NewReader(csvFile)
        records, err := reader.ReadAll()
        if err != nil {
            continue
        }
        
        // 解析数据
        for _, record := range records[1:] { // 跳过标题行
            if len(record) < 5 {
                continue
            }
            
            amount, _ := strconv.ParseFloat(record[3], 64)
            timestamp, _ := time.Parse("2006-01-02 15:04:05", record[4])
            
            dataRecord := DataRecord{
                ID:        record[0],
                UserID:    record[1],
                Type:      record[2],
                Amount:    amount,
                Timestamp: timestamp,
            }
            
            // 数据验证
            if dataRecord.Amount > 0 && dataRecord.UserID != "" {
                allRecords = append(allRecords, dataRecord)
            }
        }
    }
    
    // 数据聚合
    aggregated := make(map[string]float64)
    for _, record := range allRecords {
        key := record.UserID + "_" + record.Type
        aggregated[key] += record.Amount
    }
    
    // 批量插入数据库
    tx, err := dp.db.Begin()
    if err != nil {
        return err
    }
    defer tx.Rollback()
    
    stmt, err := tx.Prepare("INSERT INTO daily_aggregates (user_id, type, amount, date) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)")
    if err != nil {
        return err
    }
    defer stmt.Close()
    
    for key, amount := range aggregated {
        parts := strings.Split(key, "_")
        userID, dataType := parts[0], parts[1]
        
        _, err = stmt.Exec(userID, dataType, amount, date.Format("2006-01-02"))
        if err != nil {
            return err
        }
    }
    
    if err = tx.Commit(); err != nil {
        return err
    }
    
    // 生成报告并上传到 S3
    reportData := dp.generateReport(aggregated, date)
    reportKey := fmt.Sprintf("reports/daily_%s.json", date.Format("2006-01-02"))
    
    _, err = dp.s3Client.PutObject(&s3.PutObjectInput{
        Bucket: aws.String("data-reports"),
        Key:    aws.String(reportKey),
        Body:   strings.NewReader(reportData),
    })
    
    return err
}

func (dp *DataProcessor) generateReport(data map[string]float64, date time.Time) string {
    report := map[string]interface{}{
        "date":    date.Format("2006-01-02"),
        "summary": data,
        "total":   0.0,
    }
    
    var total float64
    for _, amount := range data {
        total += amount
    }
    report["total"] = total
    
    jsonData, _ := json.Marshal(report)
    return string(jsonData)
}
```

**解决方案：管道模式 + 依赖注入**

```go
// 定义数据处理接口
type DataReader interface {
    ReadData(date time.Time) ([]DataRecord, error)
}

type DataValidator interface {
    Validate(record DataRecord) bool
}

type DataAggregator interface {
    Aggregate(records []DataRecord) map[string]float64
}

type DataWriter interface {
    WriteAggregates(aggregates map[string]float64, date time.Time) error
}

type ReportGenerator interface {
    GenerateReport(aggregates map[string]float64, date time.Time) ([]byte, error)
}

type ReportUploader interface {
    UploadReport(data []byte, key string) error
}

// CSV 数据读取器
type CSVDataReader struct {
    fs      FileSystem
    baseDir string
}

func NewCSVDataReader(fs FileSystem, baseDir string) *CSVDataReader {
    return &CSVDataReader{fs: fs, baseDir: baseDir}
}

func (r *CSVDataReader) ReadData(date time.Time) ([]DataRecord, error) {
    pattern := fmt.Sprintf("%s/data_%s_*.csv", r.baseDir, date.Format("2006-01-02"))
    files, err := r.fs.Glob(pattern)
    if err != nil {
        return nil, err
    }
    
    var allRecords []DataRecord
    
    for _, file := range files {
        records, err := r.readCSVFile(file)
        if err != nil {
            continue // 跳过错误文件
        }
        allRecords = append(allRecords, records...)
    }
    
    return allRecords, nil
}

func (r *CSVDataReader) readCSVFile(filename string) ([]DataRecord, error) {
    data, err := r.fs.ReadFile(filename)
    if err != nil {
        return nil, err
    }
    
    reader := csv.NewReader(strings.NewReader(string(data)))
    records, err := reader.ReadAll()
    if err != nil {
        return nil, err
    }
    
    var dataRecords []DataRecord
    for i, record := range records {
        if i == 0 { // 跳过标题行
            continue
        }
        
        if len(record) < 5 {
            continue
        }
        
        amount, err := strconv.ParseFloat(record[3], 64)
        if err != nil {
            continue
        }
        
        timestamp, err := time.Parse("2006-01-02 15:04:05", record[4])
        if err != nil {
            continue
        }
        
        dataRecords = append(dataRecords, DataRecord{
            ID:        record[0],
            UserID:    record[1],
            Type:      record[2],
            Amount:    amount,
            Timestamp: timestamp,
        })
    }
    
    return dataRecords, nil
}

// 数据验证器
type DefaultDataValidator struct{}

func (v *DefaultDataValidator) Validate(record DataRecord) bool {
    return record.Amount > 0 && 
           record.UserID != "" && 
           record.Type != "" &&
           !record.Timestamp.IsZero()
}

// 数据聚合器
type SumAggregator struct{}

func (a *SumAggregator) Aggregate(records []DataRecord) map[string]float64 {
    aggregated := make(map[string]float64)
    
    for _, record := range records {
        key := record.UserID + "_" + record.Type
        aggregated[key] += record.Amount
    }
    
    return aggregated
}

// 数据库写入器
type DatabaseWriter struct {
    db Database
}

func NewDatabaseWriter(db Database) *DatabaseWriter {
    return &DatabaseWriter{db: db}
}

func (w *DatabaseWriter) WriteAggregates(aggregates map[string]float64, date time.Time) error {
    tx, err := w.db.Begin()
    if err != nil {
        return err
    }
    defer tx.Rollback()
    
    for key, amount := range aggregates {
        parts := strings.Split(key, "_")
        if len(parts) != 2 {
            continue
        }
        
        userID, dataType := parts[0], parts[1]
        
        _, err = tx.Exec(
            "INSERT INTO daily_aggregates (user_id, type, amount, date) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)",
            userID, dataType, amount, date.Format("2006-01-02"),
        )
        if err != nil {
            return err
        }
    }
    
    return tx.Commit()
}

// 可测试的数据处理器
type DataProcessor struct {
    reader      DataReader
    validator   DataValidator
    aggregator  DataAggregator
    writer      DataWriter
    reportGen   ReportGenerator
    uploader    ReportUploader
}

func NewDataProcessor(
    reader DataReader,
    validator DataValidator,
    aggregator DataAggregator,
    writer DataWriter,
    reportGen ReportGenerator,
    uploader ReportUploader,
) *DataProcessor {
    return &DataProcessor{
        reader:     reader,
        validator:  validator,
        aggregator: aggregator,
        writer:     writer,
        reportGen:  reportGen,
        uploader:   uploader,
    }
}

func (dp *DataProcessor) ProcessDailyData(date time.Time) error {
    // 读取数据
    records, err := dp.reader.ReadData(date)
    if err != nil {
        return fmt.Errorf("failed to read data: %w", err)
    }
    
    // 验证数据
    var validRecords []DataRecord
    for _, record := range records {
        if dp.validator.Validate(record) {
            validRecords = append(validRecords, record)
        }
    }
    
    // 聚合数据
    aggregates := dp.aggregator.Aggregate(validRecords)
    
    // 写入数据库
    if err := dp.writer.WriteAggregates(aggregates, date); err != nil {
        return fmt.Errorf("failed to write aggregates: %w", err)
    }
    
    // 生成报告
    reportData, err := dp.reportGen.GenerateReport(aggregates, date)
    if err != nil {
        return fmt.Errorf("failed to generate report: %w", err)
    }
    
    // 上传报告
    reportKey := fmt.Sprintf("reports/daily_%s.json", date.Format("2006-01-02"))
    if err := dp.uploader.UploadReport(reportData, reportKey); err != nil {
        return fmt.Errorf("failed to upload report: %w", err)
    }
    
    return nil
}

// 测试代码
func TestDataProcessor_ProcessDailyData(t *testing.T) {
    ctrl := gomock.NewController(t)
    defer ctrl.Finish()
    
    mockReader := mocks.NewMockDataReader(ctrl)
    mockValidator := mocks.NewMockDataValidator(ctrl)
    mockAggregator := mocks.NewMockDataAggregator(ctrl)
    mockWriter := mocks.NewMockDataWriter(ctrl)
    mockReportGen := mocks.NewMockReportGenerator(ctrl)
    mockUploader := mocks.NewMockReportUploader(ctrl)
    
    processor := NewDataProcessor(
        mockReader, mockValidator, mockAggregator,
        mockWriter, mockReportGen, mockUploader,
    )
    
    testDate := time.Date(2023, 1, 1, 0, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC)
    
    t.Run("successful processing", func(t *testing.T) {
        rawRecords := []DataRecord{
            {ID: "1", UserID: "user1", Type: "purchase", Amount: 100.0},
            {ID: "2", UserID: "user1", Type: "refund", Amount: -20.0}, // 无效数据
            {ID: "3", UserID: "user2", Type: "purchase", Amount: 50.0},
        }
        
        validRecords := []DataRecord{
            {ID: "1", UserID: "user1", Type: "purchase", Amount: 100.0},
            {ID: "3", UserID: "user2", Type: "purchase", Amount: 50.0},
        }
        
        aggregates := map[string]float64{
            "user1_purchase": 100.0,
            "user2_purchase": 50.0,
        }
        
        reportData := []byte(`{"date":"2023-01-01","total":150.0}`)
        
        // 设置 mock 期望
        mockReader.EXPECT().
            ReadData(testDate).
            Return(rawRecords, nil)
        
        // 验证每条记录
        mockValidator.EXPECT().Validate(rawRecords[0]).Return(true)
        mockValidator.EXPECT().Validate(rawRecords[1]).Return(false)
        mockValidator.EXPECT().Validate(rawRecords[2]).Return(true)
        
        mockAggregator.EXPECT().
            Aggregate(validRecords).
            Return(aggregates)
        
        mockWriter.EXPECT().
            WriteAggregates(aggregates, testDate).
            Return(nil)
        
        mockReportGen.EXPECT().
            GenerateReport(aggregates, testDate).
            Return(reportData, nil)
        
        mockUploader.EXPECT().
            UploadReport(reportData, "reports/daily_2023-01-01.json").
            Return(nil)
        
        err := processor.ProcessDailyData(testDate)
        
        assert.NoError(t, err)
    })
    
    t.Run("reader error", func(t *testing.T) {
        mockReader.EXPECT().
            ReadData(testDate).
            Return(nil, errors.New("file not found"))
        
        err := processor.ProcessDailyData(testDate)
        
        assert.Error(t, err)
        assert.Contains(t, err.Error(), "failed to read data")
    })
    
    t.Run("writer error", func(t *testing.T) {
        records := []DataRecord{
            {ID: "1", UserID: "user1", Type: "purchase", Amount: 100.0},
        }
        
        aggregates := map[string]float64{
            "user1_purchase": 100.0,
        }
        
        mockReader.EXPECT().ReadData(testDate).Return(records, nil)
        mockValidator.EXPECT().Validate(records[0]).Return(true)
        mockAggregator.EXPECT().Aggregate(records).Return(aggregates)
        mockWriter.EXPECT().
            WriteAggregates(aggregates, testDate).
            Return(errors.New("database error"))
        
        err := processor.ProcessDailyData(testDate)
        
        assert.Error(t, err)
        assert.Contains(t, err.Error(), "failed to write aggregates")
    })
}

// 集成测试
func TestDataProcessor_Integration(t *testing.T) {
    // 使用内存文件系统
    memFS := NewMemoryFileSystem()
    
    // 创建测试 CSV 数据
    csvData := `id,user_id,type,amount,timestamp
1,user1,purchase,100.50,2023-01-01 10:00:00
2,user1,refund,-20.00,2023-01-01 11:00:00
3,user2,purchase,75.25,2023-01-01 12:00:00`
    
    memFS.WriteFile("data/data_2023-01-01_001.csv", []byte(csvData), 0644)
    
    // 创建内存数据库
    db, err := sql.Open("sqlite3", ":memory:")
    require.NoError(t, err)
    defer db.Close()
    
    // 创建表
    _, err = db.Exec(`
        CREATE TABLE daily_aggregates (
            user_id TEXT,
            type TEXT,
            amount REAL,
            date TEXT
        )
    `)
    require.NoError(t, err)
    
    // 创建真实的组件
    reader := NewCSVDataReader(memFS, "data")
    validator := &DefaultDataValidator{}
    aggregator := &SumAggregator{}
    writer := NewDatabaseWriter(&SQLDatabase{db: db})
    reportGen := &JSONReportGenerator{}
    uploader := &MockUploader{}
    
    processor := NewDataProcessor(reader, validator, aggregator, writer, reportGen, uploader)
    
    testDate := time.Date(2023, 1, 1, 0, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC)
    
    err = processor.ProcessDailyData(testDate)
    assert.NoError(t, err)
    
    // 验证数据库中的数据
    rows, err := db.Query("SELECT user_id, type, amount FROM daily_aggregates")
    require.NoError(t, err)
    defer rows.Close()
    
    var results []struct {
        UserID string
        Type   string
        Amount float64
    }
    
    for rows.Next() {
        var result struct {
            UserID string
            Type   string
            Amount float64
        }
        err = rows.Scan(&result.UserID, &result.Type, &result.Amount)
        require.NoError(t, err)
        results = append(results, result)
    }
    
    assert.Len(t, results, 2)
    
    // 验证聚合结果
    expectedResults := map[string]float64{
        "user1_purchase": 100.50,
        "user2_purchase": 75.25,
    }
    
    for _, result := range results {
        key := result.UserID + "_" + result.Type
        expectedAmount, exists := expectedResults[key]
        assert.True(t, exists)
        assert.Equal(t, expectedAmount, result.Amount)
    }
}
```

这些例子展示了更多复杂的测试场景：


1. **状态机测试**：通过分离状态转换逻辑和副作用，使复杂的状态机变得可测试
2. **权限系统测试**：通过接口抽象和依赖注入，将认证、授权、缓存等关注点分离
3. **数据处理流程测试**：使用管道模式将复杂的ETL流程分解为可独立测试的组件

这些解决方案的共同特点是：

* **单一职责**：每个组件只负责一个明确的功能
* **依赖注入**：通过接口注入依赖，便于测试时替换
* **错误处理**：明确的错误传播和处理机制
* **可组合性**：组件可以灵活组合以适应不同的测试场景

通过这些模式，即使是最复杂的业务逻辑也能变得可测试和可维护。
