### 更多测试困难的代码例子

#### 1. 分布式锁和并发控制测试

**问题：难以测试的分布式锁**

```go
// 难以测试的分布式锁实现
type DistributedLockManager struct {
    redis   *redis.Client
    etcd    *clientv3.Client
    timeout time.Duration
}

func (dlm *DistributedLockManager) ExecuteWithLock(key string, fn func() error) error {
    // 尝试获取 Redis 锁
    lockKey := "lock:" + key
    lockValue := uuid.New().String()
    
    // 使用 Lua 脚本确保原子性
    luaScript := `
        if redis.call("get", KEYS[1]) == false then
            return redis.call("setex", KEYS[1], ARGV[1], ARGV[2])
        else
            return 0
        end
    `
    
    result, err := dlm.redis.Eval(context.Background(), luaScript, []string{lockKey}, 
        int(dlm.timeout.Seconds()), lockValue).Result()
    if err != nil || result.(int64) == 0 {
        return errors.New("failed to acquire lock")
    }
    
    // 设置自动续期
    ctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(context.Background())
    defer cancel()
    
    go func() {
        ticker := time.NewTicker(dlm.timeout / 3)
        defer ticker.Stop()
        
        for {
            select {
            case <-ctx.Done():
                return
            case <-ticker.C:
                // 续期锁
                extendScript := `
                    if redis.call("get", KEYS[1]) == ARGV[1] then
                        return redis.call("expire", KEYS[1], ARGV[2])
                    else
                        return 0
                    end
                `
                dlm.redis.Eval(context.Background(), extendScript, []string{lockKey}, 
                    lockValue, int(dlm.timeout.Seconds()))
            }
        }
    }()
    
    // 执行业务逻辑
    defer func() {
        // 释放锁
        releaseScript := `
            if redis.call("get", KEYS[1]) == ARGV[1] then
                return redis.call("del", KEYS[1])
            else
                return 0
            end
        `
        dlm.redis.Eval(context.Background(), releaseScript, []string{lockKey}, lockValue)
    }()
    
    // 同时在 etcd 中记录锁信息用于监控
    etcdKey := "/locks/" + key
    lease, err := dlm.etcd.Grant(context.Background(), int64(dlm.timeout.Seconds()))
    if err == nil {
        dlm.etcd.Put(context.Background(), etcdKey, lockValue, clientv3.WithLease(lease.ID))
        defer dlm.etcd.Delete(context.Background(), etcdKey)
    }
    
    return fn()
}
```

**解决方案：抽象锁接口 + 可控时间**

```go
// 定义锁接口
type DistributedLock interface {
    TryLock(ctx context.Context, key string, ttl time.Duration) (LockHandle, error)
}

type LockHandle interface {
    Unlock(ctx context.Context) error
    Extend(ctx context.Context, ttl time.Duration) error
    IsValid(ctx context.Context) bool
}

// 定义脚本执行器接口
type ScriptExecutor interface {
    Eval(ctx context.Context, script string, keys []string, args ...interface{}) (interface{}, error)
}

// 定义租约管理器接口
type LeaseManager interface {
    Grant(ctx context.Context, key string, ttl time.Duration) (string, error)
    Revoke(ctx context.Context, leaseID string) error
}

// Redis 锁实现
type RedisLock struct {
    executor ScriptExecutor
    clock    Clock
}

func NewRedisLock(executor ScriptExecutor, clock Clock) *RedisLock {
    return &RedisLock{
        executor: executor,
        clock:    clock,
    }
}

func (r *RedisLock) TryLock(ctx context.Context, key string, ttl time.Duration) (LockHandle, error) {
    lockKey := "lock:" + key
    lockValue := generateLockValue()
    
    script := `
        if redis.call("get", KEYS[1]) == false then
            return redis.call("setex", KEYS[1], ARGV[1], ARGV[2])
        else
            return 0
        end
    `
    
    result, err := r.executor.Eval(ctx, script, []string{lockKey}, 
        int(ttl.Seconds()), lockValue)
    if err != nil {
        return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to execute lock script: %w", err)
    }
    
    if result.(int64) == 0 {
        return nil, errors.New("lock already held")
    }
    
    return &RedisLockHandle{
        executor:  r.executor,
        clock:     r.clock,
        key:       lockKey,
        value:     lockValue,
        ttl:       ttl,
        acquired:  r.clock.Now(),
    }, nil
}

type RedisLockHandle struct {
    executor ScriptExecutor
    clock    Clock
    key      string
    value    string
    ttl      time.Duration
    acquired time.Time
}

func (h *RedisLockHandle) Unlock(ctx context.Context) error {
    script := `
        if redis.call("get", KEYS[1]) == ARGV[1] then
            return redis.call("del", KEYS[1])
        else
            return 0
        end
    `
    
    _, err := h.executor.Eval(ctx, script, []string{h.key}, h.value)
    return err
}

func (h *RedisLockHandle) Extend(ctx context.Context, ttl time.Duration) error {
    script := `
        if redis.call("get", KEYS[1]) == ARGV[1] then
            return redis.call("expire", KEYS[1], ARGV[2])
        else
            return 0
        end
    `
    
    result, err := h.executor.Eval(ctx, script, []string{h.key}, h.value, int(ttl.Seconds()))
    if err != nil {
        return err
    }
    
    if result.(int64) == 0 {
        return errors.New("lock no longer held")
    }
    
    h.ttl = ttl
    return nil
}

func (h *RedisLockHandle) IsValid(ctx context.Context) bool {
    // 检查是否超过 TTL
    if h.clock.Now().Sub(h.acquired) > h.ttl {
        return false
    }
    
    // 可以添加额外的验证逻辑
    return true
}

// 可测试的分布式锁管理器
type DistributedLockManager struct {
    lock         DistributedLock
    leaseManager LeaseManager
    clock        Clock
}

func NewDistributedLockManager(
    lock DistributedLock,
    leaseManager LeaseManager,
    clock Clock,
) *DistributedLockManager {
    return &DistributedLockManager{
        lock:         lock,
        leaseManager: leaseManager,
        clock:        clock,
    }
}

func (dlm *DistributedLockManager) ExecuteWithLock(
    ctx context.Context,
    key string,
    ttl time.Duration,
    fn func() error,
) error {
    // 获取锁
    handle, err := dlm.lock.TryLock(ctx, key, ttl)
    if err != nil {
        return fmt.Errorf("failed to acquire lock: %w", err)
    }
    
    // 确保释放锁
    defer func() {
        if unlockErr := handle.Unlock(ctx); unlockErr != nil {
            log.Printf("Failed to unlock: %v", unlockErr)
        }
    }()
    
    // 启动自动续期
    renewCtx, cancelRenew := context.WithCancel(ctx)
    defer cancelRenew()
    
    go dlm.autoRenew(renewCtx, handle, ttl)
    
    // 记录租约信息
    leaseID, err := dlm.leaseManager.Grant(ctx, key, ttl)
    if err == nil {
        defer dlm.leaseManager.Revoke(ctx, leaseID)
    }
    
    // 执行业务逻辑
    return fn()
}

func (dlm *DistributedLockManager) autoRenew(ctx context.Context, handle LockHandle, ttl time.Duration) {
    ticker := time.NewTicker(ttl / 3)
    defer ticker.Stop()
    
    for {
        select {
        case <-ctx.Done():
            return
        case <-ticker.C:
            if !handle.IsValid(ctx) {
                return
            }
            
            if err := handle.Extend(ctx, ttl); err != nil {
                log.Printf("Failed to extend lock: %v", err)
                return
            }
        }
    }
}

// 测试代码
func TestDistributedLockManager_ExecuteWithLock(t *testing.T) {
    ctrl := gomock.NewController(t)
    defer ctrl.Finish()
    
    mockLock := mocks.NewMockDistributedLock(ctrl)
    mockLeaseManager := mocks.NewMockLeaseManager(ctrl)
    mockClock := &MockClock{current: time.Date(2023, 1, 1, 12, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC)}
    
    dlm := NewDistributedLockManager(mockLock, mockLeaseManager, mockClock)
    
    t.Run("successful execution", func(t *testing.T) {
        mockHandle := mocks.NewMockLockHandle(ctrl)
        ctx := context.Background()
        key := "test-resource"
        ttl := 30 * time.Second
        
        executed := false
        fn := func() error {
            executed = true
            return nil
        }
        
        // 设置期望
        mockLock.EXPECT().
            TryLock(ctx, key, ttl).
            Return(mockHandle, nil)
        
        mockLeaseManager.EXPECT().
            Grant(ctx, key, ttl).
            Return("lease-123", nil)
        
        // 自动续期相关的期望
        mockHandle.EXPECT().
            IsValid(ctx).
            Return(true).
            AnyTimes()
        
        mockHandle.EXPECT().
            Extend(ctx, ttl).
            Return(nil).
            AnyTimes()
        
        mockHandle.EXPECT().
            Unlock(ctx).
            Return(nil)
        
        mockLeaseManager.EXPECT().
            Revoke(ctx, "lease-123").
            Return(nil)
        
        err := dlm.ExecuteWithLock(ctx, key, ttl, fn)
        
        assert.NoError(t, err)
        assert.True(t, executed)
    })
    
    t.Run("lock acquisition failure", func(t *testing.T) {
        ctx := context.Background()
        key := "test-resource"
        ttl := 30 * time.Second
        
        executed := false
        fn := func() error {
            executed = true
            return nil
        }
        
        mockLock.EXPECT().
            TryLock(ctx, key, ttl).
            Return(nil, errors.New("lock already held"))
        
        err := dlm.ExecuteWithLock(ctx, key, ttl, fn)
        
        assert.Error(t, err)
        assert.False(t, executed)
        assert.Contains(t, err.Error(), "failed to acquire lock")
    })
    
    t.Run("function execution error", func(t *testing.T) {
        mockHandle := mocks.NewMockLockHandle(ctrl)
        ctx := context.Background()
        key := "test-resource"
        ttl := 30 * time.Second
        
        fnError := errors.New("business logic error")
        fn := func() error {
            return fnError
        }
        
        mockLock.EXPECT().
            TryLock(ctx, key, ttl).
            Return(mockHandle, nil)
        
        mockLeaseManager.EXPECT().
            Grant(ctx, key, ttl).
            Return("lease-123", nil)
        
        mockHandle.EXPECT().
            IsValid(ctx).
            Return(true).
            AnyTimes()
        
        mockHandle.EXPECT().
            Extend(ctx, ttl).
            Return(nil).
            AnyTimes()
        
        mockHandle.EXPECT().
            Unlock(ctx).
            Return(nil)
        
        mockLeaseManager.EXPECT().
            Revoke(ctx, "lease-123").
            Return(nil)
        
        err := dlm.ExecuteWithLock(ctx, key, ttl, fn)
        
        assert.Error(t, err)
        assert.Equal(t, fnError, err)
    })
}

// 并发测试
func TestDistributedLockManager_Concurrency(t *testing.T) {
    // 使用内存实现进行并发测试
    memExecutor := NewMemoryScriptExecutor()
    memLeaseManager := NewMemoryLeaseManager()
    realClock := &RealClock{}
    
    lock := NewRedisLock(memExecutor, realClock)
    dlm := NewDistributedLockManager(lock, memLeaseManager, realClock)
    
    const numGoroutines = 10
    const numIterations = 100
    
    var counter int64
    var wg sync.WaitGroup
    
    for i := 0; i < numGoroutines; i++ {
        wg.Add(1)
        go func() {
            defer wg.Done()
            
            for j := 0; j < numIterations; j++ {
                err := dlm.ExecuteWithLock(
                    context.Background(),
                    "counter",
                    time.Second,
                    func() error {
                        // 模拟一些工作
                        time.Sleep(time.Millisecond)
                        atomic.AddInt64(&counter, 1)
                        return nil
                    },
                )
                
                if err != nil {
                    // 在高并发情况下，一些锁获取失败是正常的
                    continue
                }
            }
        }()
    }
    
    wg.Wait()
    
    // 验证计数器的值（应该小于等于总迭代次数，因为有些锁获取会失败）
    finalCounter := atomic.LoadInt64(&counter)
    assert.True(t, finalCounter > 0)
    assert.True(t, finalCounter <= numGoroutines*numIterations)
    
    t.Logf("Successfully executed %d out of %d operations", 
        finalCounter, numGoroutines*numIterations)
}
```

#### 2. 复杂的事件溯源和CQRS测试

**问题：难以测试的事件溯源系统**

```go
// 难以测试的事件溯源实现
type EventStore struct {
    db        *sql.DB
    publisher *kafka.Producer
    snapshots map[string]interface{}
}

func (es *EventStore) SaveEvents(aggregateID string, events []Event, expectedVersion int) error {
    tx, err := es.db.Begin()
    if err != nil {
        return err
    }
    defer tx.Rollback()
    
    // 检查版本冲突
    var currentVersion int
    err = tx.QueryRow("SELECT COALESCE(MAX(version), 0) FROM events WHERE aggregate_id = ?", 
        aggregateID).Scan(&currentVersion)
    if err != nil {
        return err
    }
    
    if currentVersion != expectedVersion {
        return errors.New("version conflict")
    }
    
    // 保存事件
    for i, event := range events {
        eventData, _ := json.Marshal(event.Data)
        metadata, _ := json.Marshal(event.Metadata)
        
        _, err = tx.Exec(`
            INSERT INTO events (aggregate_id, version, event_type, event_data, metadata, created_at)
            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`,
            aggregateID, expectedVersion+i+1, event.Type, eventData, metadata, time.Now())
        if err != nil {
            return err
        }
        
        // 发布事件到 Kafka
        message := &kafka.Message{
            Topic: "domain-events",
            Key:   []byte(aggregateID),
            Value: eventData,
            Headers: []kafka.Header{
                {Key: "event-type", Value: []byte(event.Type)},
                {Key: "aggregate-id", Value: []byte(aggregateID)},
                {Key: "version", Value: []byte(strconv.Itoa(expectedVersion + i + 1))},
            },
        }
        
        es.publisher.Produce(message, nil)
    }
    
    if err = tx.Commit(); err != nil {
        return err
    }
    
    // 检查是否需要创建快照
    newVersion := expectedVersion + len(events)
    if newVersion%10 == 0 { // 每10个事件创建一个快照
        go es.createSnapshot(aggregateID, newVersion)
    }
    
    return nil
}

func (es *EventStore) LoadEvents(aggregateID string, fromVersion int) ([]Event, error) {
    // 先尝试从快照加载
    if snapshot, exists := es.snapshots[aggregateID]; exists {
        // 从快照版本开始加载事件
        snapshotVersion := snapshot.(map[string]interface{})["version"].(int)
        if snapshotVersion >= fromVersion {
            fromVersion = snapshotVersion + 1
        }
    }
    
    rows, err := es.db.Query(`
        SELECT version, event_type, event_data, metadata, created_at
        FROM events 
        WHERE aggregate_id = ? AND version >= ?
        ORDER BY version`, aggregateID, fromVersion)
    if err != nil {
        return nil, err
    }
    defer rows.Close()
    
    var events []Event
    for rows.Next() {
        var event Event
        var eventData, metadata []byte
        
        err = rows.Scan(&event.Version, &event.Type, &eventData, &metadata, &event.CreatedAt)
        if err != nil {
            return nil, err
        }
        
        json.Unmarshal(eventData, &event.Data)
        json.Unmarshal(metadata, &event.Metadata)
        
        events = append(events, event)
    }
    
    return events, nil
}

func (es *EventStore) createSnapshot(aggregateID string, version int) {
    // 重建聚合状态
    events, err := es.LoadEvents(aggregateID, 0)
    if err != nil {
        return
    }
    
    // 应用所有事件重建状态
    var aggregate interface{}
    for _, event := range events {
        aggregate = applyEvent(aggregate, event)
    }
    
    // 保存快照
    es.snapshots[aggregateID] = map[string]interface{}{
        "version":   version,
        "aggregate": aggregate,
    }
}
```

**解决方案：分离关注点 + 事件总线抽象**

```go
// 定义事件存储接口
type EventStore interface {
    SaveEvents(ctx context.Context, streamID string, events []Event, expectedVersion int) error
    LoadEvents(ctx context.Context, streamID string, fromVersion int) ([]Event, error)
    LoadSnapshot(ctx context.Context, streamID string) (*Snapshot, error)
    SaveSnapshot(ctx context.Context, snapshot *Snapshot) error
}

// 定义事件发布器接口
type EventPublisher interface {
    Publish(ctx context.Context, events []Event) error
}

// 定义快照策略接口
type SnapshotStrategy interface {
    ShouldCreateSnapshot(streamID string, version int) bool
}

// 定义聚合重建器接口
type AggregateRebuilder interface {
    Rebuild(events []Event) (interface{}, error)
}

// 事件存储实现
type SQLEventStore struct {
    db    Database
    clock Clock
}

func NewSQLEventStore(db Database, clock Clock) *SQLEventStore {
    return &SQLEventStore{db: db, clock: clock}
}

func (s *SQLEventStore) SaveEvents(ctx context.Context, streamID string, events []Event, expectedVersion int) error {
    tx, err := s.db.BeginTx(ctx)
    if err != nil {
        return fmt.Errorf("failed to begin transaction: %w", err)
    }
    defer tx.Rollback()
    
    // 检查版本冲突
    currentVersion, err := s.getCurrentVersion(ctx, tx, streamID)
    if err != nil {
        return fmt.Errorf("failed to get current version: %w", err)
    }
    
    if currentVersion != expectedVersion {
        return &VersionConflictError{
            Expected: expectedVersion,
            Actual:   currentVersion,
        }
    }
    
    // 保存事件
    for i, event := range events {
        if err := s.saveEvent(ctx, tx, streamID, expectedVersion+i+1, event); err != nil {
            return fmt.Errorf("failed to save event: %w", err)
        }
    }
    
    return tx.Commit()
}

func (s *SQLEventStore) LoadEvents(ctx context.Context, streamID string, fromVersion int) ([]Event, error) {
    query := `
        SELECT version, event_type, event_data, metadata, created_at
        FROM events 
        WHERE stream_id = ? AND version >= ?
        ORDER BY version
    `
    
    rows, err := s.db.QueryContext(ctx, query, streamID, fromVersion)
    if err != nil {
        return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to query events: %w", err)
    }
    defer rows.Close()
    
    var events []Event
    for rows.Next() {
        event, err := s.scanEvent(rows)
        if err != nil {
            return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to scan event: %w", err)
        }
        events = append(events, event)
    }
    
    return events, nil
}

func (s *SQLEventStore) getCurrentVersion(ctx context.Context, tx Transaction, streamID string) (int, error) {
    var version int
    err := tx.QueryRowContext(ctx, 
        "SELECT COALESCE(MAX(version), 0) FROM events WHERE stream_id = ?", 
        streamID).Scan(&version)
    return version, err
}

func (s *SQLEventStore) saveEvent(ctx context.Context, tx Transaction, streamID string, version int, event Event) error {
    eventData, err := json.Marshal(event.Data)
    if err != nil {
        return err
    }
    
    metadata, err := json.Marshal(event.Metadata)
    if err != nil {
        return err
    }
    
    _, err = tx.ExecContext(ctx, `
        INSERT INTO events (stream_id, version, event_type, event_data, metadata, created_at)
        VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`,
        streamID, version, event.Type, eventData, metadata, s.clock.Now())
    
    return err
}

// 可测试的事件溯源服务
type EventSourcingService struct {
    eventStore        EventStore
    eventPublisher    EventPublisher
    snapshotStrategy  SnapshotStrategy
    aggregateRebuilder AggregateRebuilder
}

func NewEventSourcingService(
    eventStore EventStore,
    eventPublisher EventPublisher,
    snapshotStrategy SnapshotStrategy,
    aggregateRebuilder AggregateRebuilder,
) *EventSourcingService {
    return &EventSourcingService{
        eventStore:         eventStore,
        eventPublisher:     eventPublisher,
        snapshotStrategy:   snapshotStrategy,
        aggregateRebuilder: aggregateRebuilder,
    }
}

func (s *EventSourcingService) SaveEvents(ctx context.Context, streamID string, events []Event, expectedVersion int) error {
    // 保存事件到存储
    if err := s.eventStore.SaveEvents(ctx, streamID, events, expectedVersion); err != nil {
        return fmt.Errorf("failed to save events: %w", err)
    }
    
    // 发布事件
    if err := s.eventPublisher.Publish(ctx, events); err != nil {
        // 记录错误但不失败，因为事件已经保存
        log.Printf("Failed to publish events: %v", err)
    }
    
    // 检查是否需要创建快照
    newVersion := expectedVersion + len(events)
    if s.snapshotStrategy.ShouldCreateSnapshot(streamID, newVersion) {
        go s.createSnapshotAsync(context.Background(), streamID, newVersion)
    }
    
    return nil
}

func (s *EventSourcingService) LoadAggregate(ctx context.Context, streamID string) (interface{}, int, error) {
    var fromVersion int
    var aggregate interface{}
    
    // 尝试加载快照
    snapshot, err := s.eventStore.LoadSnapshot(ctx, streamID)
    if err == nil && snapshot != nil {
        aggregate = snapshot.Data
        fromVersion = snapshot.Version + 1
    }
    
    // 加载事件
    events, err := s.eventStore.LoadEvents(ctx, streamID, fromVersion)
    if err != nil {
        return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to load events: %w", err)
    }
    
    // 重建聚合
    if len(events) > 0 {
        rebuiltAggregate, err := s.aggregateRebuilder.Rebuild(events)
        if err != nil {
            return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to rebuild aggregate: %w", err)
        }
        
        if aggregate == nil {
            aggregate = rebuiltAggregate
        } else {
            // 将快照状态与新事件合并
            aggregate = s.mergeAggregateState(aggregate, rebuiltAggregate)
        }
    }
    
    version := fromVersion + len(events) - 1
    if snapshot != nil {
        version = max(snapshot.Version, version)
    }
    
    return aggregate, version, nil
}

func (s *EventSourcingService) createSnapshotAsync(ctx context.Context, streamID string, version int) {
    aggregate, _, err := s.LoadAggregate(ctx, streamID)
    if err != nil {
        log.Printf("Failed to load aggregate for snapshot: %v", err)
        return
    }
    
    snapshot := &Snapshot{
        StreamID: streamID,
        Version:  version,
        Data:     aggregate,
        CreatedAt: time.Now(),
    }
    
    if err := s.eventStore.SaveSnapshot(ctx, snapshot); err != nil {
        log.Printf("Failed to save snapshot: %v", err)
    }
}

// 测试代码
func TestEventSourcingService_SaveEvents(t *testing.T) {
    ctrl := gomock.NewController(t)
    defer ctrl.Finish()
    
    mockEventStore := mocks.NewMockEventStore(ctrl)
    mockEventPublisher := mocks.NewMockEventPublisher(ctrl)
    mockSnapshotStrategy := mocks.NewMockSnapshotStrategy(ctrl)
    mockAggregateRebuilder := mocks.NewMockAggregateRebuilder(ctrl)
    
    service := NewEventSourcingService(
        mockEventStore,
        mockEventPublisher,
        mockSnapshotStrategy,
        mockAggregateRebuilder,
    )
    
    t.Run("successful save", func(t *testing.T) {
        ctx := context.Background()
        streamID := "user-123"
        events := []Event{
            {Type: "UserCreated", Data: map[string]interface{}{"name": "John"}},
            {Type: "UserEmailChanged", Data: map[string]interface{}{"email": "john@example.com"}},
        }
        expectedVersion := 0
        
        mockEventStore.EXPECT().
            SaveEvents(ctx, streamID, events, expectedVersion).
            Return(nil)
        
        mockEventPublisher.EXPECT().
            Publish(ctx, events).
            Return(nil)
        
        mockSnapshotStrategy.EXPECT().
            ShouldCreateSnapshot(streamID, 2).
            Return(false)
        
        err := service.SaveEvents(ctx, streamID, events, expectedVersion)
        
        assert.NoError(t, err)
    })
    
    t.Run("version conflict", func(t *testing.T) {
        ctx := context.Background()
        streamID := "user-123"
        events := []Event{
            {Type: "UserCreated", Data: map[string]interface{}{"name": "John"}},
        }
        expectedVersion := 0
        
        versionError := &VersionConflictError{Expected: 0, Actual: 2}
        
        mockEventStore.EXPECT().
            SaveEvents(ctx, streamID, events, expectedVersion).
            Return(versionError)
        
        err := service.SaveEvents(ctx, streamID, events, expectedVersion)
        
        assert.Error(t, err)
        assert.Contains(t, err.Error(), "failed to save events")
    })
    
    t.Run("should create snapshot", func(t *testing.T) {
        ctx := context.Background()
        streamID := "user-123"
        events := []Event{
            {Type: "UserCreated", Data: map[string]interface{}{"name": "John"}},
        }
        expectedVersion := 9 // 第10个事件
        
        mockEventStore.EXPECT().
            SaveEvents(ctx, streamID, events, expectedVersion).
            Return(nil)
        
        mockEventPublisher.EXPECT().
            Publish(ctx, events).
            Return(nil)
        
        mockSnapshotStrategy.EXPECT().
            ShouldCreateSnapshot(streamID, 10).
            Return(true)
        
        // 快照创建是异步的，我们需要等待一下
        mockEventStore.EXPECT().
            LoadSnapshot(gomock.Any(), streamID).
            Return(nil, errors.New("no snapshot")).
            AnyTimes()
        
        mockEventStore.EXPECT().
            LoadEvents(gomock.Any(), streamID, 0).
            Return(events, nil).
            AnyTimes()
        
        mockAggregateRebuilder.EXPECT().
            Rebuild(events).
            Return(map[string]interface{}{"name": "John"}, nil).
            AnyTimes()
        
        mockEventStore.EXPECT().
            SaveSnapshot(gomock.Any(), gomock.Any()).
            Return(nil).
            AnyTimes()
        
        err := service.SaveEvents(ctx, streamID, events, expectedVersion)
        
        assert.NoError(t, err)
        
        // 等待异步快照创建
        time.Sleep(100 * time.Millisecond)
    })
}

func TestEventSourcingService_LoadAggregate(t *testing.T) {
    ctrl := gomock.NewController(t)
    defer ctrl.Finish()
    
    mockEventStore := mocks.NewMockEventStore(ctrl)
    mockEventPublisher := mocks.NewMockEventPublisher(ctrl)
    mockSnapshotStrategy := mocks.NewMockSnapshotStrategy(ctrl)
    mockAggregateRebuilder := mocks.NewMockAggregateRebuilder(ctrl)
    
    service := NewEventSourcingService(
        mockEventStore,
        mockEventPublisher,
        mockSnapshotStrategy,
        mockAggregateRebuilder,
    )
    
    t.Run("load from events only", func(t *testing.T) {
        ctx := context.Background()
        streamID := "user-123"
        
        events := []Event{
            {Type: "UserCreated", Data: map[string]interface{}{"name": "John"}},
            {Type: "UserEmailChanged", Data: map[string]interface{}{"email": "john@example.com"}},
        }
        
        expectedAggregate := &User{
            ID:    "123",
            Name:  "John",
            Email: "john@example.com",
        }
        
        mockEventStore.EXPECT().
            LoadSnapshot(ctx, streamID).
            Return(nil, errors.New("no snapshot"))
        
        mockEventStore.EXPECT().
            LoadEvents(ctx, streamID, 0).
            Return(events, nil)
        
        mockAggregateRebuilder.EXPECT().
            Rebuild(events).
            Return(expectedAggregate, nil)
        
        aggregate, version, err := service.LoadAggregate(ctx, streamID)
        
        assert.NoError(t, err)
        assert.Equal(t, expectedAggregate, aggregate)
        assert.Equal(t, 1, version) // fromVersion(0) + len(events)(2) - 1
    })
    
    t.Run("load from snapshot and events", func(t *testing.T) {
        ctx := context.Background()
        streamID := "user-123"
        
        snapshot := &Snapshot{
            StreamID:  streamID,
            Version:   5,
            Data:      &User{ID: "123", Name: "John"},
            CreatedAt: time.Now(),
        }
        
        newEvents := []Event{
            {Type: "UserEmailChanged", Data: map[string]interface{}{"email": "john@example.com"}},
        }
        
        expectedAggregate := &User{
            ID:    "123",
            Name:  "John",
            Email: "john@example.com",
        }
        
        mockEventStore.EXPECT().
            LoadSnapshot(ctx, streamID).
            Return(snapshot, nil)
        
        mockEventStore.EXPECT().
            LoadEvents(ctx, streamID, 6). // snapshot.Version + 1
            Return(newEvents, nil)
        
        mockAggregateRebuilder.EXPECT().
            Rebuild(newEvents).
            Return(&User{Email: "john@example.com"}, nil)
        
        aggregate, version, err := service.LoadAggregate(ctx, streamID)
        
        assert.NoError(t, err)
        assert.Equal(t, 6, version) // max(snapshot.Version, fromVersion + len(events) - 1)
        // 这里需要实现 mergeAggregateState 的逻辑
    })
}
```

#### 3. 复杂的流处理和实时计算测试

**问题：难以测试的流处理系统**

```go
// 难以测试的流处理器
type StreamProcessor struct {
    kafkaConsumer *kafka.Consumer
    redisClient   *redis.Client
    influxClient  *influxdb.Client
    alertManager  *AlertManager
}

func (sp *StreamProcessor) ProcessUserActivityStream() {
    sp.kafkaConsumer.Subscribe("user-activity", nil)
    
    // 滑动窗口状态
    windows := make(map[string]*TimeWindow)
    
    for {
        msg, err := sp.kafkaConsumer.ReadMessage(-1)
        if err != nil {
            continue
        }
        
        var activity UserActivity
        json.Unmarshal(msg.Value, &activity)
        
        // 更新用户活动计数
        userKey := "activity:" + activity.UserID
        sp.redisClient.Incr(context.Background(), userKey)
        sp.redisClient.Expire(context.Background(), userKey, time.Hour)
        
        // 滑动窗口计算
        windowKey := activity.UserID + ":" + activity.Type
        window, exists := windows[windowKey]
        if !exists {
            window = &TimeWindow{
                Start:  time.Now().Truncate(time.Minute),
                Events: make([]UserActivity, 0),
            }
            windows[windowKey] = window
        }
        
        // 清理过期事件
        now := time.Now()
        var validEvents []UserActivity
        for _, event := range window.Events {
            if now.Sub(event.Timestamp) < 5*time.Minute {
                validEvents = append(validEvents, event)
            }
        }
        window.Events = validEvents
        window.Events = append(window.Events, activity)
        
        // 检查异常模式
        if len(window.Events) > 100 { // 5分钟内超过100个事件
            alert := Alert{
                Type:    "HIGH_ACTIVITY",
                UserID:  activity.UserID,
                Message: fmt.Sprintf("User %s has %d activities in 5 minutes", 
                    activity.UserID, len(window.Events)),
                Timestamp: now,
            }
            sp.alertManager.SendAlert(alert)
        }
        
        // 计算实时指标
        metrics := sp.calculateMetrics(window.Events)
        
        // 写入 InfluxDB
        point := influxdb.NewPoint(
            "user_activity_metrics",
            map[string]string{
                "user_id": activity.UserID,
                "type":    activity.Type,
            },
            map[string]interface{}{
                "count":     len(window.Events),
                "avg_value": metrics.AvgValue,
                "max_value": metrics.MaxValue,
            },
            now,
        )
        
        bp := influxdb.NewBatchPoints(influxdb.BatchPointsConfig{
            Database: "analytics",
        })
        bp.AddPoint(point)
        sp.influxClient.Write(bp)
        
        // 清理旧窗口
        if len(windows) > 10000 {
            sp.cleanupOldWindows(windows)
        }
    }
}

func (sp *StreamProcessor) calculateMetrics(events []UserActivity) Metrics {
    if len(events) == 0 {
        return Metrics{}
    }
    
    var sum, max float64
    for _, event := range events {
        sum += event.Value
        if event.Value > max {
            max = event.Value
        }
    }
    
    return Metrics{
        AvgValue: sum / float64(len(events)),
        MaxValue: max,
    }
}
```

**解决方案：流处理管道 + 事件驱动架构**

```go
// 定义流处理接口
type MessageConsumer interface {
    Subscribe(topics []string) error
    ReadMessage(ctx context.Context) (Message, error)
    Close() error
}

type StateStore interface {
    Get(ctx context.Context, key string) (interface{}, error)
    Set(ctx context.Context, key string, value interface{}, ttl time.Duration) error
    Increment(ctx context.Context, key string) (int64, error)
}

type MetricsWriter interface {
    WriteMetrics(ctx context.Context, metrics []Metric) error
}

type AlertSender interface {
    SendAlert(ctx context.Context, alert Alert) error
}

type WindowManager interface {
    AddEvent(windowKey string, event interface{}) *Window
    GetWindow(windowKey string) *Window
    CleanupExpired(before time.Time)
}

// 滑动窗口管理器
type SlidingWindowManager struct {
    windows    map[string]*Window
    windowSize time.Duration
    mu         sync.RWMutex
}

func NewSlidingWindowManager(windowSize time.Duration) *SlidingWindowManager {
    return &SlidingWindowManager{
        windows:    make(map[string]*Window),
        windowSize: windowSize,
    }
}

func (wm *SlidingWindowManager) AddEvent(windowKey string, event interface{}) *Window {
    wm.mu.Lock()
    defer wm.mu.Unlock()
    
    window, exists := wm.windows[windowKey]
    if !exists {
        window = &Window{
            Key:    windowKey,
            Events: make([]interface{}, 0),
            Start:  time.Now(),
        }
        wm.windows[windowKey] = window
    }
    
    // 清理过期事件
    now := time.Now()
    var validEvents []interface{}
    for _, e := range window.Events {
        if event, ok := e.(TimestampedEvent); ok {
            if now.Sub(event.GetTimestamp()) < wm.windowSize {
                validEvents = append(validEvents, e)
            }
        }
    }
    
    window.Events = validEvents
    window.Events = append(window.Events, event)
    window.LastUpdated = now
    
    return window
}

func (wm *SlidingWindowManager) GetWindow(windowKey string) *Window {
    wm.mu.RLock()
    defer wm.mu.RUnlock()
    
    return wm.windows[windowKey]
}

func (wm *SlidingWindowManager) CleanupExpired(before time.Time) {
    wm.mu.Lock()
    defer wm.mu.Unlock()
    
    for key, window := range wm.windows {
        if window.LastUpdated.Before(before) {
            delete(wm.windows, key)
        }
    }
}

// 事件处理器接口
type EventProcessor interface {
    Process(ctx context.Context, event interface{}) error
}

// 活动计数处理器
type ActivityCountProcessor struct {
    stateStore StateStore
}

func NewActivityCountProcessor(stateStore StateStore) *ActivityCountProcessor {
    return &ActivityCountProcessor{stateStore: stateStore}
}

func (p *ActivityCountProcessor) Process(ctx context.Context, event interface{}) error {
    activity, ok := event.(*UserActivity)
    if !ok {
        return errors.New("invalid event type")
    }
    
    userKey := "activity:" + activity.UserID
    _, err := p.stateStore.Increment(ctx, userKey)
    if err != nil {
        return fmt.Errorf("failed to increment activity count: %w", err)
    }
    
    return p.stateStore.Set(ctx, userKey+"_ttl", time.Now(), time.Hour)
}

// 异常检测处理器
type AnomalyDetectionProcessor struct {
    windowManager WindowManager
    alertSender   AlertSender
    threshold     int
}

func NewAnomalyDetectionProcessor(
    windowManager WindowManager,
    alertSender AlertSender,
    threshold int,
) *AnomalyDetectionProcessor {
    return &AnomalyDetectionProcessor{
        windowManager: windowManager,
        alertSender:   alertSender,
        threshold:     threshold,
    }
}

func (p *AnomalyDetectionProcessor) Process(ctx context.Context, event interface{}) error {
    activity, ok := event.(*UserActivity)
    if !ok {
        return errors.New("invalid event type")
    }
    
    windowKey := activity.UserID + ":" + activity.Type
    window := p.windowManager.AddEvent(windowKey, activity)
    
    if len(window.Events) > p.threshold {
        alert := Alert{
            Type:      "HIGH_ACTIVITY",
            UserID:    activity.UserID,
            Message:   fmt.Sprintf("User %s has %d activities in window", activity.UserID, len(window.Events)),
            Timestamp: time.Now(),
        }
        
        return p.alertSender.SendAlert(ctx, alert)
    }
    
    return nil
}

// 指标计算处理器
type MetricsCalculationProcessor struct {
    windowManager WindowManager
    metricsWriter MetricsWriter
}

func NewMetricsCalculationProcessor(
    windowManager WindowManager,
    metricsWriter MetricsWriter,
) *MetricsCalculationProcessor {
    return &MetricsCalculationProcessor{
        windowManager: windowManager,
        metricsWriter: metricsWriter,
    }
}

func (p *MetricsCalculationProcessor) Process(ctx context.Context, event interface{}) error {
    activity, ok := event.(*UserActivity)
    if !ok {
        return errors.New("invalid event type")
    }
    
    windowKey := activity.UserID + ":" + activity.Type
    window := p.windowManager.GetWindow(windowKey)
    if window == nil {
        return nil
    }
    
    metrics := p.calculateMetrics(window.Events)
    
    metric := Metric{
        Name:      "user_activity_metrics",
        Tags:      map[string]string{"user_id": activity.UserID, "type": activity.Type},
        Fields:    map[string]interface{}{"count": len(window.Events), "avg_value": metrics.AvgValue},
        Timestamp: time.Now(),
    }
    
    return p.metricsWriter.WriteMetrics(ctx, []Metric{metric})
}

func (p *MetricsCalculationProcessor) calculateMetrics(events []interface{}) Metrics {
    if len(events) == 0 {
        return Metrics{}
    }
    
    var sum, max float64
    count := 0
    
    for _, e := range events {
        if activity, ok := e.(*UserActivity); ok {
            sum += activity.Value
            if activity.Value > max {
                max = activity.Value
            }
            count++
        }
    }
    
    if count == 0 {
        return Metrics{}
    }
    
    return Metrics{
        AvgValue: sum / float64(count),
        MaxValue: max,
    }
}

// 可测试的流处理器
type StreamProcessor struct {
    consumer   MessageConsumer
    processors []EventProcessor
    clock      Clock
}

func NewStreamProcessor(
    consumer MessageConsumer,
    processors []EventProcessor,
    clock Clock,
) *StreamProcessor {
    return &StreamProcessor{
        consumer:   consumer,
        processors: processors,
        clock:      clock,
    }
}

func (sp *StreamProcessor) Start(ctx context.Context) error {
    if err := sp.consumer.Subscribe([]string{"user-activity"}); err != nil {
        return fmt.Errorf("failed to subscribe: %w", err)
    }
    
    for {
        select {
        case <-ctx.Done():
            return ctx.Err()
        default:
            if err := sp.processMessage(ctx); err != nil {
                log.Printf("Failed to process message: %v", err)
                continue
            }
        }
    }
}

func (sp *StreamProcessor) processMessage(ctx context.Context) error {
    msg, err := sp.consumer.ReadMessage(ctx)
    if err != nil {
        return fmt.Errorf("failed to read message: %w", err)
    }
    
    var activity UserActivity
    if err := json.Unmarshal(msg.Value(), &activity); err != nil {
        return fmt.Errorf("failed to unmarshal message: %w", err)
    }
    
    // 处理事件通过所有处理器
    for _, processor := range sp.processors {
        if err := processor.Process(ctx, &activity); err != nil {
            log.Printf("Processor failed: %v", err)
            // 继续处理其他处理器
        }
    }
    
    return nil
}

// 测试代码
func TestStreamProcessor_ProcessMessage(t *testing.T) {
    ctrl := gomock.NewController(t)
    defer ctrl.Finish()
    
    mockConsumer := mocks.NewMockMessageConsumer(ctrl)
    mockProcessor1 := mocks.NewMockEventProcessor(ctrl)
    mockProcessor2 := mocks.NewMockEventProcessor(ctrl)
    mockClock := &MockClock{current: time.Date(2023, 1, 1, 12, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC)}
    
    processor := NewStreamProcessor(
        mockConsumer,
        []EventProcessor{mockProcessor1, mockProcessor2},
        mockClock,
    )
    
    t.Run("successful processing", func(t *testing.T) {
        ctx := context.Background()
        
        activity := &UserActivity{
            UserID:    "user123",
            Type:      "click",
            Value:     1.0,
            Timestamp: mockClock.current,
        }
        
        activityJSON, _ := json.Marshal(activity)
        mockMessage := &MockMessage{value: activityJSON}
        
        mockConsumer.EXPECT().
            Subscribe([]string{"user-activity"}).
            Return(nil)
        
        mockConsumer.EXPECT().
            ReadMessage(ctx).
            Return(mockMessage, nil).
            Times(1)
        
        mockProcessor1.EXPECT().
            Process(ctx, activity).
            Return(nil)
        
        mockProcessor2.EXPECT().
            Process(ctx, activity).
            Return(nil)
        
        // 模拟上下文取消来停止处理
        cancelCtx, cancel := context.WithCancel(ctx)
        cancel()
        
        mockConsumer.EXPECT().
            ReadMessage(cancelCtx).
            Return(nil, cancelCtx.Err()).
            AnyTimes()
        
        err := processor.Start(cancelCtx)
        assert.Error(t, err)
        assert.Equal(t, context.Canceled, err)
    })
    
    t.Run("processor error should not stop processing", func(t *testing.T) {
        ctx := context.Background()
        
        activity := &UserActivity{
            UserID:    "user123",
            Type:      "click",
            Value:     1.0,
            Timestamp: mockClock.current,
        }
        
        activityJSON, _ := json.Marshal(activity)
        mockMessage := &MockMessage{value: activityJSON}
        
        mockConsumer.EXPECT().
            Subscribe([]string{"user-activity"}).
            Return(nil)
        
        mockConsumer.EXPECT().
            ReadMessage(ctx).
            Return(mockMessage, nil).
            Times(1)
        
        // 第一个处理器失败
        mockProcessor1.EXPECT().
            Process(ctx, activity).
            Return(errors.New("processor error"))
        
        // 第二个处理器仍然应该被调用
        mockProcessor2.EXPECT().
            Process(ctx, activity).
            Return(nil)
        
        cancelCtx, cancel := context.WithCancel(ctx)
        cancel()
        
        mockConsumer.EXPECT().
            ReadMessage(cancelCtx).
            Return(nil, cancelCtx.Err()).
            AnyTimes()
        
        err := processor.Start(cancelCtx)
        assert.Error(t, err)
        assert.Equal(t, context.Canceled, err)
    })
}

func TestAnomalyDetectionProcessor_Process(t *testing.T) {
    ctrl := gomock.NewController(t)
    defer ctrl.Finish()
    
    mockWindowManager := mocks.NewMockWindowManager(ctrl)
    mockAlertSender := mocks.NewMockAlertSender(ctrl)
    
    processor := NewAnomalyDetectionProcessor(mockWindowManager, mockAlertSender, 5)
    
    t.Run("normal activity", func(t *testing.T) {
        ctx := context.Background()
        activity := &UserActivity{
            UserID: "user123",
            Type:   "click",
            Value:  1.0,
        }
        
        window := &Window{
            Key:    "user123:click",
            Events: make([]interface{}, 3), // 少于阈值
        }
        
        mockWindowManager.EXPECT().
            AddEvent("user123:click", activity).
            Return(window)
        
        err := processor.Process(ctx, activity)
        
        assert.NoError(t, err)
    })
    
    t.Run("anomaly detected", func(t *testing.T) {
        ctx := context.Background()
        activity := &UserActivity{
            UserID: "user123",
            Type:   "click",
            Value:  1.0,
        }
        
        window := &Window{
            Key:    "user123:click",
            Events: make([]interface{}, 10), // 超过阈值
        }
        
        mockWindowManager.EXPECT().
            AddEvent("user123:click", activity).
            Return(window)
        
        mockAlertSender.EXPECT().
            SendAlert(ctx, gomock.Any()).
            DoAndReturn(func(ctx context.Context, alert Alert) error {
                assert.Equal(t, "HIGH_ACTIVITY", alert.Type)
                assert.Equal(t, "user123", alert.UserID)
                assert.Contains(t, alert.Message, "10 activities")
                return nil
            })
        
        err := processor.Process(ctx, activity)
        
        assert.NoError(t, err)
    })
}

// 集成测试
func TestStreamProcessor_Integration(t *testing.T) {
    // 创建内存实现
    memConsumer := NewMemoryMessageConsumer()
    memStateStore := NewMemoryStateStore()
    memMetricsWriter := NewMemoryMetricsWriter()
    memAlertSender := NewMemoryAlertSender()
    
    windowManager := NewSlidingWindowManager(5 * time.Minute)
    
    // 创建处理器
    processors := []EventProcessor{
        NewActivityCountProcessor(memStateStore),
        NewAnomalyDetectionProcessor(windowManager, memAlertSender, 3),
        NewMetricsCalculationProcessor(windowManager, memMetricsWriter),
    }
    
    streamProcessor := NewStreamProcessor(memConsumer, processors, &RealClock{})
    
    // 启动处理器
    ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), time.Second)
    defer cancel()
    
    go streamProcessor.Start(ctx)
    
    // 发送测试消息
    activities := []*UserActivity{
        {UserID: "user1", Type: "click", Value: 1.0, Timestamp: time.Now()},
        {UserID: "user1", Type: "click", Value: 2.0, Timestamp: time.Now()},
        {UserID: "user1", Type: "click", Value: 3.0, Timestamp: time.Now()},
        {UserID: "user1", Type: "click", Value: 4.0, Timestamp: time.Now()}, // 触发异常
    }
    
    for _, activity := range activities {
        data, _ := json.Marshal(activity)
        memConsumer.SendMessage(&MemoryMessage{
            topic: "user-activity",
            value: data,
        })
    }
    
    // 等待处理完成
    time.Sleep(100 * time.Millisecond)
    
    // 验证结果
    
    // 检查活动计数
    count, err := memStateStore.Get(context.Background(), "activity:user1")
    assert.NoError(t, err)
    assert.Equal(t, int64(4), count)
    
    // 检查异常告警
    alerts := memAlertSender.GetAlerts()
    assert.Len(t, alerts, 1)
    assert.Equal(t, "HIGH_ACTIVITY", alerts[0].Type)
    assert.Equal(t, "user1", alerts[0].UserID)
    
    // 检查指标
    metrics := memMetricsWriter.GetMetrics()
    assert.NotEmpty(t, metrics)
    
    lastMetric := metrics[len(metrics)-1]
    assert.Equal(t, "user_activity_metrics", lastMetric.Name)
    assert.Equal(t, "user1", lastMetric.Tags["user_id"])
    assert.Equal(t, "click", lastMetric.Tags["type"])
    assert.Equal(t, 4, lastMetric.Fields["count"])
}
```

这些例子展示了更多复杂的测试场景：


1. **分布式锁测试**：通过抽象锁接口和脚本执行器，使复杂的分布式锁逻辑变得可测试
2. **事件溯源测试**：通过分离事件存储、发布、快照等关注点，使复杂的事件溯源系统变得可测试
3. **流处理测试**：通过事件驱动架构和处理器管道，使复杂的实时流处理变得可测试

这些解决方案的核心思想是：

* **接口抽象**：为所有外部依赖定义清晰的接口
* **关注点分离**：将复杂的业务逻辑分解为独立的组件
* **依赖注入**：通过构造函数注入所有依赖
* **可控时间**：抽象时间依赖，使时间相关的逻辑可测试
* **事件驱动**：使用事件驱动架构提高组件间的解耦

通过这些模式，即使是最复杂的分布式系统和实时处理逻辑也能变得可测试和可维护。

当然！让我提供更多极其复杂的测试场景：

### 更复杂的测试场景

#### 1. 多租户微服务架构测试

**问题：难以测试的多租户系统**

```go
// 难以测试的多租户微服务
type MultiTenantService struct {
    dbConnections map[string]*sql.DB  // 每个租户一个数据库
    cacheClients  map[string]*redis.Client
    configManager *ConfigManager
    circuitBreaker *CircuitBreaker
    rateLimiter   *RateLimiter
    auditLogger   *AuditLogger
    encryption    *EncryptionService
}

func (mts *MultiTenantService) ProcessTenantRequest(ctx context.Context, tenantID string, request *Request) (*Response, error) {
    // 获取租户配置
    tenantConfig, err := mts.configManager.GetTenantConfig(tenantID)
    if err != nil {
        return nil, err
    }
    
    // 检查租户是否激活
    if !tenantConfig.IsActive {
        return nil, errors.New("tenant is not active")
    }
    
    // 速率限制检查
    if !mts.rateLimiter.Allow(tenantID, request.UserID) {
        mts.auditLogger.LogRateLimitExceeded(tenantID, request.UserID)
        return nil, errors.New("rate limit exceeded")
    }
    
    // 获取租户特定的数据库连接
    db, exists := mts.dbConnections[tenantID]
    if !exists {
        // 动态创建数据库连接
        dsn := fmt.Sprintf("host=%s port=%d user=%s password=%s dbname=%s",
            tenantConfig.Database.Host,
            tenantConfig.Database.Port,
            tenantConfig.Database.User,
            mts.encryption.Decrypt(tenantConfig.Database.EncryptedPassword),
            tenantConfig.Database.Name)
        
        db, err = sql.Open("postgres", dsn)
        if err != nil {
            return nil, err
        }
        mts.dbConnections[tenantID] = db
    }
    
    // 获取租户特定的缓存客户端
    cache, exists := mts.cacheClients[tenantID]
    if !exists {
        cache = redis.NewClient(&redis.Options{
            Addr:     tenantConfig.Cache.Address,
            Password: mts.encryption.Decrypt(tenantConfig.Cache.EncryptedPassword),
            DB:       tenantConfig.Cache.Database,
        })
        mts.cacheClients[tenantID] = cache
    }
    
    // 使用熔断器保护下游服务调用
    result, err := mts.circuitBreaker.Execute(func() (interface{}, error) {
        return mts.processBusinessLogic(ctx, tenantID, db, cache, request)
    })
    
    if err != nil {
        mts.auditLogger.LogError(tenantID, request.UserID, err)
        return nil, err
    }
    
    response := result.(*Response)
    
    // 记录审计日志
    mts.auditLogger.LogSuccess(tenantID, request.UserID, request.Action, response.ID)
    
    return response, nil
}

func (mts *MultiTenantService) processBusinessLogic(ctx context.Context, tenantID string, db *sql.DB, cache *redis.Client, request *Request) (*Response, error) {
    // 复杂的业务逻辑，涉及多个数据库操作、缓存操作、外部API调用等
    
    // 检查缓存
    cacheKey := fmt.Sprintf("tenant:%s:user:%s:action:%s", tenantID, request.UserID, request.Action)
    cachedResult, err := cache.Get(ctx, cacheKey).Result()
    if err == nil {
        var response Response
        json.Unmarshal([]byte(cachedResult), &response)
        return &response, nil
    }
    
    // 开始数据库事务
    tx, err := db.BeginTx(ctx, nil)
    if err != nil {
        return nil, err
    }
    defer tx.Rollback()
    
    // 复杂的数据库操作
    var userID int
    err = tx.QueryRowContext(ctx, "SELECT id FROM users WHERE tenant_id = ? AND external_id = ?", 
        tenantID, request.UserID).Scan(&userID)
    if err != nil {
        return nil, err
    }
    
    // 调用外部服务
    externalResponse, err := mts.callExternalService(ctx, tenantID, request)
    if err != nil {
        return nil, err
    }
    
    // 更新数据库
    _, err = tx.ExecContext(ctx, 
        "INSERT INTO actions (tenant_id, user_id, action_type, external_response, created_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)",
        tenantID, userID, request.Action, externalResponse, time.Now())
    if err != nil {
        return nil, err
    }
    
    if err = tx.Commit(); err != nil {
        return nil, err
    }
    
    response := &Response{
        ID:     generateID(),
        Status: "success",
        Data:   externalResponse,
    }
    
    // 缓存结果
    responseData, _ := json.Marshal(response)
    cache.Set(ctx, cacheKey, responseData, time.Hour)
    
    return response, nil
}

func (mts *MultiTenantService) callExternalService(ctx context.Context, tenantID string, request *Request) (string, error) {
    // 调用外部API，可能需要租户特定的认证信息
    client := &http.Client{Timeout: 30 * time.Second}
    
    req, err := http.NewRequestWithContext(ctx, "POST", "https://external-api.com/process", 
        strings.NewReader(request.Data))
    if err != nil {
        return "", err
    }
    
    // 添加租户特定的认证头
    tenantConfig, _ := mts.configManager.GetTenantConfig(tenantID)
    req.Header.Set("Authorization", "Bearer "+mts.encryption.Decrypt(tenantConfig.ExternalAPI.Token))
    req.Header.Set("X-Tenant-ID", tenantID)
    
    resp, err := client.Do(req)
    if err != nil {
        return "", err
    }
    defer resp.Body.Close()
    
    if resp.StatusCode != 200 {
        return "", fmt.Errorf("external API returned status %d", resp.StatusCode)
    }
    
    body, err := io.ReadAll(resp.Body)
    return string(body), err
}
```

**解决方案：多层抽象 + 租户上下文**

```go
// 定义租户上下文
type TenantContext struct {
    TenantID string
    Config   *TenantConfig
    Database Database
    Cache    Cache
    Logger   Logger
}

// 定义租户管理器接口
type TenantManager interface {
    GetTenantContext(ctx context.Context, tenantID string) (*TenantContext, error)
    ReleaseTenantContext(tenantID string) error
}

// 定义业务逻辑处理器接口
type BusinessLogicProcessor interface {
    Process(ctx context.Context, tenantCtx *TenantContext, request *Request) (*Response, error)
}

// 定义外部服务客户端接口
type ExternalServiceClient interface {
    Call(ctx context.Context, tenantCtx *TenantContext, request *Request) (string, error)
}

// 定义访问控制接口
type AccessController interface {
    CheckRateLimit(tenantID, userID string) error
    CheckPermissions(tenantCtx *TenantContext, userID string, action string) error
}

// 定义审计接口
type AuditService interface {
    LogRequest(tenantID, userID, action string) error
    LogResponse(tenantID, userID string, response *Response) error
    LogError(tenantID, userID string, err error) error
}

// 租户管理器实现
type DefaultTenantManager struct {
    configManager   ConfigManager
    dbFactory      DatabaseFactory
    cacheFactory   CacheFactory
    encryptionSvc  EncryptionService
    tenantContexts sync.Map // 缓存租户上下文
}

func NewDefaultTenantManager(
    configManager ConfigManager,
    dbFactory DatabaseFactory,
    cacheFactory CacheFactory,
    encryptionSvc EncryptionService,
) *DefaultTenantManager {
    return &DefaultTenantManager{
        configManager: configManager,
        dbFactory:     dbFactory,
        cacheFactory:  cacheFactory,
        encryptionSvc: encryptionSvc,
    }
}

func (tm *DefaultTenantManager) GetTenantContext(ctx context.Context, tenantID string) (*TenantContext, error) {
    // 尝试从缓存获取
    if cached, ok := tm.tenantContexts.Load(tenantID); ok {
        return cached.(*TenantContext), nil
    }
    
    // 获取租户配置
    config, err := tm.configManager.GetTenantConfig(tenantID)
    if err != nil {
        return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get tenant config: %w", err)
    }
    
    if !config.IsActive {
        return nil, errors.New("tenant is not active")
    }
    
    // 创建数据库连接
    dbConfig := DatabaseConfig{
        Host:     config.Database.Host,
        Port:     config.Database.Port,
        User:     config.Database.User,
        Password: tm.encryptionSvc.Decrypt(config.Database.EncryptedPassword),
        DBName:   config.Database.Name,
    }
    
    db, err := tm.dbFactory.CreateDatabase(dbConfig)
    if err != nil {
        return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to create database connection: %w", err)
    }
    
    // 创建缓存连接
    cacheConfig := CacheConfig{
        Address:  config.Cache.Address,
        Password: tm.encryptionSvc.Decrypt(config.Cache.EncryptedPassword),
        Database: config.Cache.Database,
    }
    
    cache, err := tm.cacheFactory.CreateCache(cacheConfig)
    if err != nil {
        return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to create cache connection: %w", err)
    }
    
    // 创建租户特定的日志器
    logger := NewTenantLogger(tenantID)
    
    tenantCtx := &TenantContext{
        TenantID: tenantID,
        Config:   config,
        Database: db,
        Cache:    cache,
        Logger:   logger,
    }
    
    // 缓存租户上下文
    tm.tenantContexts.Store(tenantID, tenantCtx)
    
    return tenantCtx, nil
}

// 业务逻辑处理器实现
type DefaultBusinessLogicProcessor struct {
    externalClient ExternalServiceClient
}

func NewDefaultBusinessLogicProcessor(externalClient ExternalServiceClient) *DefaultBusinessLogicProcessor {
    return &DefaultBusinessLogicProcessor{
        externalClient: externalClient,
    }
}

func (p *DefaultBusinessLogicProcessor) Process(ctx context.Context, tenantCtx *TenantContext, request *Request) (*Response, error) {
    // 检查缓存
    cacheKey := fmt.Sprintf("tenant:%s:user:%s:action:%s", tenantCtx.TenantID, request.UserID, request.Action)
    
    var cachedResponse Response
    if err := tenantCtx.Cache.Get(ctx, cacheKey, &cachedResponse); err == nil {
        return &cachedResponse, nil
    }
    
    // 开始数据库事务
    tx, err := tenantCtx.Database.BeginTx(ctx)
    if err != nil {
        return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to begin transaction: %w", err)
    }
    defer tx.Rollback()
    
    // 查询用户
    var userID int
    err = tx.QueryRowContext(ctx, 
        "SELECT id FROM users WHERE tenant_id = ? AND external_id = ?", 
        tenantCtx.TenantID, request.UserID).Scan(&userID)
    if err != nil {
        return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to find user: %w", err)
    }
    
    // 调用外部服务
    externalResponse, err := p.externalClient.Call(ctx, tenantCtx, request)
    if err != nil {
        return nil, fmt.Errorf("external service call failed: %w", err)
    }
    
    // 保存操作记录
    _, err = tx.ExecContext(ctx,
        "INSERT INTO actions (tenant_id, user_id, action_type, external_response, created_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)",
        tenantCtx.TenantID, userID, request.Action, externalResponse, time.Now())
    if err != nil {
        return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to save action: %w", err)
    }
    
    if err = tx.Commit(); err != nil {
        return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to commit transaction: %w", err)
    }
    
    response := &Response{
        ID:     generateID(),
        Status: "success",
        Data:   externalResponse,
    }
    
    // 缓存结果
    tenantCtx.Cache.Set(ctx, cacheKey, response, time.Hour)
    
    return response, nil
}

// 可测试的多租户服务
type MultiTenantService struct {
    tenantManager    TenantManager
    businessProcessor BusinessLogicProcessor
    accessController AccessController
    auditService     AuditService
    circuitBreaker   CircuitBreaker
}

func NewMultiTenantService(
    tenantManager TenantManager,
    businessProcessor BusinessLogicProcessor,
    accessController AccessController,
    auditService AuditService,
    circuitBreaker CircuitBreaker,
) *MultiTenantService {
    return &MultiTenantService{
        tenantManager:     tenantManager,
        businessProcessor: businessProcessor,
        accessController:  accessController,
        auditService:      auditService,
        circuitBreaker:    circuitBreaker,
    }
}

func (mts *MultiTenantService) ProcessTenantRequest(ctx context.Context, tenantID string, request *Request) (*Response, error) {
    // 记录请求审计
    if err := mts.auditService.LogRequest(tenantID, request.UserID, request.Action); err != nil {
        log.Printf("Failed to log request: %v", err)
    }
    
    // 获取租户上下文
    tenantCtx, err := mts.tenantManager.GetTenantContext(ctx, tenantID)
    if err != nil {
        mts.auditService.LogError(tenantID, request.UserID, err)
        return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get tenant context: %w", err)
    }
    
    // 访问控制检查
    if err := mts.accessController.CheckRateLimit(tenantID, request.UserID); err != nil {
        mts.auditService.LogError(tenantID, request.UserID, err)
        return nil, fmt.Errorf("access denied: %w", err)
    }
    
    if err := mts.accessController.CheckPermissions(tenantCtx, request.UserID, request.Action); err != nil {
        mts.auditService.LogError(tenantID, request.UserID, err)
        return nil, fmt.Errorf("permission denied: %w", err)
    }
    
    // 使用熔断器执行业务逻辑
    result, err := mts.circuitBreaker.Execute(func() (interface{}, error) {
        return mts.businessProcessor.Process(ctx, tenantCtx, request)
    })
    
    if err != nil {
        mts.auditService.LogError(tenantID, request.UserID, err)
        return nil, fmt.Errorf("business logic failed: %w", err)
    }
    
    response := result.(*Response)
    
    // 记录成功审计
    mts.auditService.LogResponse(tenantID, request.UserID, response)
    
    return response, nil
}

// 测试代码
func TestMultiTenantService_ProcessTenantRequest(t *testing.T) {
    ctrl := gomock.NewController(t)
    defer ctrl.Finish()
    
    mockTenantManager := mocks.NewMockTenantManager(ctrl)
    mockBusinessProcessor := mocks.NewMockBusinessLogicProcessor(ctrl)
    mockAccessController := mocks.NewMockAccessController(ctrl)
    mockAuditService := mocks.NewMockAuditService(ctrl)
    mockCircuitBreaker := mocks.NewMockCircuitBreaker(ctrl)
    
    service := NewMultiTenantService(
        mockTenantManager,
        mockBusinessProcessor,
        mockAccessController,
        mockAuditService,
        mockCircuitBreaker,
    )
    
    t.Run("successful request processing", func(t *testing.T) {
        ctx := context.Background()
        tenantID := "tenant-123"
        request := &Request{
            UserID: "user-456",
            Action: "create_order",
            Data:   "order data",
        }
        
        tenantCtx := &TenantContext{
            TenantID: tenantID,
            Config:   &TenantConfig{IsActive: true},
        }
        
        expectedResponse := &Response{
            ID:     "response-789",
            Status: "success",
            Data:   "processed data",
        }
        
        // 设置期望
        mockAuditService.EXPECT().
            LogRequest(tenantID, request.UserID, request.Action).
            Return(nil)
        
        mockTenantManager.EXPECT().
            GetTenantContext(ctx, tenantID).
            Return(tenantCtx, nil)
        
        mockAccessController.EXPECT().
            CheckRateLimit(tenantID, request.UserID).
            Return(nil)
        
        mockAccessController.EXPECT().
            CheckPermissions(tenantCtx, request.UserID, request.Action).
            Return(nil)
        
        mockCircuitBreaker.EXPECT().
            Execute(gomock.Any()).
            DoAndReturn(func(fn func() (interface{}, error)) (interface{}, error) {
                return fn() // 直接执行函数
            })
        
        mockBusinessProcessor.EXPECT().
            Process(ctx, tenantCtx, request).
            Return(expectedResponse, nil)
        
        mockAuditService.EXPECT().
            LogResponse(tenantID, request.UserID, expectedResponse).
            Return(nil)
        
        response, err := service.ProcessTenantRequest(ctx, tenantID, request)
        
        assert.NoError(t, err)
        assert.Equal(t, expectedResponse, response)
    })
    
    t.Run("tenant context error", func(t *testing.T) {
        ctx := context.Background()
        tenantID := "inactive-tenant"
        request := &Request{
            UserID: "user-456",
            Action: "create_order",
        }
        
        tenantError := errors.New("tenant is not active")
        
        mockAuditService.EXPECT().
            LogRequest(tenantID, request.UserID, request.Action).
            Return(nil)
        
        mockTenantManager.EXPECT().
            GetTenantContext(ctx, tenantID).
            Return(nil, tenantError)
        
        mockAuditService.EXPECT().
            LogError(tenantID, request.UserID, tenantError).
            Return(nil)
        
        response, err := service.ProcessTenantRequest(ctx, tenantID, request)
        
        assert.Error(t, err)
        assert.Nil(t, response)
        assert.Contains(t, err.Error(), "failed to get tenant context")
    })
    
    t.Run("rate limit exceeded", func(t *testing.T) {
        ctx := context.Background()
        tenantID := "tenant-123"
        request := &Request{
            UserID: "user-456",
            Action: "create_order",
        }
        
        tenantCtx := &TenantContext{
            TenantID: tenantID,
            Config:   &TenantConfig{IsActive: true},
        }
        
        rateLimitError := errors.New("rate limit exceeded")
        
        mockAuditService.EXPECT().
            LogRequest(tenantID, request.UserID, request.Action).
            Return(nil)
        
        mockTenantManager.EXPECT().
            GetTenantContext(ctx, tenantID).
            Return(tenantCtx, nil)
        
        mockAccessController.EXPECT().
            CheckRateLimit(tenantID, request.UserID).
            Return(rateLimitError)
        
        mockAuditService.EXPECT().
            LogError(tenantID, request.UserID, rateLimitError).
            Return(nil)
        
        response, err := service.ProcessTenantRequest(ctx, tenantID, request)
        
        assert.Error(t, err)
        assert.Nil(t, response)
        assert.Contains(t, err.Error(), "access denied")
    })
    
    t.Run("circuit breaker open", func(t *testing.T) {
        ctx := context.Background()
        tenantID := "tenant-123"
        request := &Request{
            UserID: "user-456",
            Action: "create_order",
        }
        
        tenantCtx := &TenantContext{
            TenantID: tenantID,
            Config:   &TenantConfig{IsActive: true},
        }
        
        circuitBreakerError := errors.New("circuit breaker is open")
        
        mockAuditService.EXPECT().LogRequest(tenantID, request.UserID, request.Action).Return(nil)
        mockTenantManager.EXPECT().GetTenantContext(ctx, tenantID).Return(tenantCtx, nil)
        mockAccessController.EXPECT().CheckRateLimit(tenantID, request.UserID).Return(nil)
        mockAccessController.EXPECT().CheckPermissions(tenantCtx, request.UserID, request.Action).Return(nil)
        
        mockCircuitBreaker.EXPECT().
            Execute(gomock.Any()).
            Return(nil, circuitBreakerError)
        
        mockAuditService.EXPECT().
            LogError(tenantID, request.UserID, circuitBreakerError).
            Return(nil)
        
        response, err := service.ProcessTenantRequest(ctx, tenantID, request)
        
        assert.Error(t, err)
        assert.Nil(t, response)
        assert.Contains(t, err.Error(), "business logic failed")
    })
}
```
